========== lib\models\plant.dart ==========

/// Structured medicinal-plant model for Jeevan Sutra.
///
/// Traditional knowledge, scientific evidence, and safety information are
/// deliberately stored separately so the app can clearly distinguish them.
class Plant {
  final String id;
  final String commonName;
  final String? sanskritName;
  final String? hindiName;
  final String scientificName;
  final String family;
  final String category;
  final String? medicinalClassification;
  final String? growingInformation;
  final String? imageAsset;
  final String identification;
  final List<String> partsUsed;
  final List<String> traditionalReferences;
  final List<String> traditionalUses;
  final List<String> scientificEvidence;
  final List<String> knownCompounds;
  final String evidenceLevel;
  final List<String> evidenceLimitations;
  final String safetySummary;
  final List<String> precautions;
  final List<String> lookAlikes;
  final bool? isToxic;
  final String? toxicityWarning;
  final String organismType;
  final double? confidence;
  final List<String> sources;
  final List<PlantReference> sourceDetails;
  final String reviewStatus;
  final String? reviewedBy;
  final String? reviewDate;
  final String contentVersion;
  final List<String> keywords;

  String get botanicalName => scientificName;
  String? get taste =>
      traditionalReferences.isNotEmpty ? traditionalReferences.first : null;
  String? get potency =>
      knownCompounds.isNotEmpty ? knownCompounds.first : null;
  String? get postDigestiveEffect => evidenceLevel;
  String? get doshaImpact => keywords.isNotEmpty ? keywords.first : null;
  String? get primaryUses =>
      traditionalUses.isNotEmpty ? traditionalUses.join(', ') : null;
  String? get contraindications =>
      precautions.isNotEmpty ? precautions.join(', ') : null;
  String? get botanicalFamily => family;

  const Plant({
    required this.id,
    required this.commonName,
    this.sanskritName,
    this.hindiName,
    required this.scientificName,
    this.family = '',
    this.category = 'General',
    this.medicinalClassification,
    this.growingInformation,
    this.imageAsset,
    this.identification = '',
    this.partsUsed = const [],
    this.traditionalReferences = const [],
    this.traditionalUses = const [],
    this.scientificEvidence = const [],
    this.knownCompounds = const [],
    this.evidenceLevel = 'not_assessed',
    this.evidenceLimitations = const [],
    this.safetySummary = '',
    this.precautions = const [],
    this.lookAlikes = const [],
    this.isToxic,
    this.toxicityWarning,
    this.organismType = 'plant',
    this.confidence,
    this.sources = const [],
    this.sourceDetails = const [],
    this.reviewStatus = 'draft',
    this.reviewedBy,
    this.reviewDate,
    this.contentVersion = '1.0',
    this.keywords = const [],
  });

  factory Plant.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return Plant(
      id: json['id'] as String,
      commonName: json['commonName'] as String,
      sanskritName: json['sanskritName'] as String?,
      hindiName: json['hindiName'] as String?,
      scientificName: json['scientificName'] as String,
      family: json['family'] as String? ?? '',
      category: json['category'] as String? ?? 'General',
      medicinalClassification: json['medicinalClassification'] as String?,
      growingInformation: json['growingInformation'] as String?,
      imageAsset: json['imageAsset'] as String?,
      identification: json['identification'] as String? ?? '',
      partsUsed: _stringList(json['partsUsed']),
      traditionalReferences: _stringList(json['traditionalReferences']),
      traditionalUses: _stringList(json['traditionalUses']),
      scientificEvidence: _stringList(json['scientificEvidence']),
      knownCompounds: _stringList(json['knownCompounds']),
      evidenceLevel: json['evidenceLevel'] as String? ?? 'not_assessed',
      evidenceLimitations: _stringList(json['evidenceLimitations']),
      safetySummary: json['safetySummary'] as String? ?? '',
      precautions: _stringList(json['precautions']),
      lookAlikes: _stringList(json['lookAlikes']),
      isToxic: json['isToxic'] as bool?,
      toxicityWarning: json['toxicityWarning'] as String?,
      organismType: json['organismType'] as String? ?? 'plant',
      confidence: (json['confidence'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
      sources: _stringList(json['sources']),
      sourceDetails:
          (json['sourceDetails'] as List?)
              ?.whereType<Map>()
              .map(
                (item) =>
                    PlantReference.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(item)),
              )
              .toList() ??
          const [],
      reviewStatus: json['reviewStatus'] as String? ?? 'draft',
      reviewedBy: json['reviewedBy'] as String?,
      reviewDate: json['reviewDate'] as String?,
      contentVersion: json['contentVersion'] as String? ?? '1.0',
      keywords: _stringList(json['keywords']),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    'id': id,
    'commonName': commonName,
    'sanskritName': sanskritName,
    'hindiName': hindiName,
    'scientificName': scientificName,
    'family': family,
    'category': category,
    'medicinalClassification': medicinalClassification,
    'growingInformation': growingInformation,
    'imageAsset': imageAsset,
    'identification': identification,
    'partsUsed': partsUsed,
    'traditionalReferences': traditionalReferences,
    'traditionalUses': traditionalUses,
    'scientificEvidence': scientificEvidence,
    'knownCompounds': knownCompounds,
    'evidenceLevel': evidenceLevel,
    'evidenceLimitations': evidenceLimitations,
    'safetySummary': safetySummary,
    'precautions': precautions,
    'lookAlikes': lookAlikes,
    'isToxic': isToxic,
    'toxicityWarning': toxicityWarning,
    'organismType': organismType,
    'confidence': confidence,
    'sources': sources,
    'sourceDetails': sourceDetails.map((source) => source.toJson()).toList(),
    'reviewStatus': reviewStatus,
    'reviewedBy': reviewedBy,
    'reviewDate': reviewDate,
    'contentVersion': contentVersion,
    'keywords': keywords,
  };

  Plant copyWithToxicity({required bool? isToxic, String? toxicityWarning}) =>
      Plant.fromJson({
        ...toJson(),
        'isToxic': isToxic,
        'toxicityWarning': toxicityWarning,
      });

  static List<String> _stringList(dynamic value) {
    if (value is! List) return const [];
    return value.whereType<String>().toList();
  }
}

/// A structured reference attached to a plant record.
class PlantReference {
  final String id;
  final String title;
  final String? authors;
  final String? organization;
  final int? year;
  final String sourceType;
  final String supports;
  final String? doi;
  final String? url;

  const PlantReference({
    required this.id,
    required this.title,
    this.authors,
    this.organization,
    this.year,
    this.sourceType = 'other',
    this.supports = '',
    this.doi,
    this.url,
  });

  factory PlantReference.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return PlantReference(
      id: json['id'] as String? ?? '',
      title: json['title'] as String? ?? '',
      authors: json['authors'] as String?,
      organization: json['organization'] as String?,
      year: json['year'] is int ? json['year'] as int : null,
      sourceType: json['sourceType'] as String? ?? 'other',
      supports: json['supports'] as String? ?? '',
      doi: json['doi'] as String?,
      url: json['url'] as String?,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    'id': id,
    'title': title,
    'authors': authors,
    'organization': organization,
    'year': year,
    'sourceType': sourceType,
    'supports': supports,
    'doi': doi,
    'url': url,
  };
}

    );

========== lib\repositories\content_repository.dart ==========

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:firebase_core/firebase_core.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

import '../models/plant.dart';

/// Loads plant knowledge from Firestore when available and falls back to the
/// bundled catalogue. Firestore's native persistent cache supports previously
/// downloaded documents while offline; the bundled JSON is the first-run
/// fallback when no Firestore cache exists yet.
class ContentRepository {
  ContentRepository._();

  static const String _plantsAsset = 'assets/knowledge/plants.json';
  static const String _plantsCollection = 'plants';

  static List<Plant>? _plantsCache;
  static bool _lastLoadUsedCloud = false;

  /// True when the most recent successful load came from Firestore (including
  /// Firestore's local persistent cache).
  static bool get lastLoadUsedCloud => _lastLoadUsedCloud;

  /// Loads plants. Set [refresh] to bypass the in-memory cache and check
  /// Firestore again. If Firestore is unavailable or empty, bundled JSON is
  /// used. Existing callers need no changes.
  static Future<List<Plant>> loadPlants({bool refresh = false}) async {
    if (!refresh && _plantsCache != null) {
      return List.unmodifiable(_plantsCache!);
    }

    final cloudPlants = await _tryLoadFirestorePlants();
    if (cloudPlants != null && cloudPlants.isNotEmpty) {
      _plantsCache = cloudPlants;
      _lastLoadUsedCloud = true;
      return List.unmodifiable(cloudPlants);
    }

    final bundledPlants = await _loadBundledPlants();
    _plantsCache = bundledPlants;
    _lastLoadUsedCloud = false;
    return List.unmodifiable(bundledPlants);
  }

  /// Forces a Firestore refresh. Returns true if a non-empty cloud/cache
  /// catalogue was read; otherwise leaves the bundled catalogue in place.
  static Future<bool> refreshFromCloud() async {
    final cloudPlants = await _tryLoadFirestorePlants();
    if (cloudPlants == null || cloudPlants.isEmpty) return false;
    _plantsCache = cloudPlants;
    _lastLoadUsedCloud = true;
    return true;
  }

  static Future<List<Plant>?> _tryLoadFirestorePlants() async {
    // Tests and early startup can call the repository before Firebase init.
    if (Firebase.apps.isEmpty) return null;

    try {
      final snapshot = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection(_plantsCollection)
          .get();
      if (snapshot.docs.isEmpty) return null;

      final plants = <Plant>[];
      for (final document in snapshot.docs) {
        final data = Map<String, dynamic>.from(document.data());
        data['id'] ??= document.id;
        plants.add(Plant.fromJson(data));
      }
      return plants;
    } on Object {
      // Network, permission, and missing-database errors should not prevent
      // the demonstration or offline catalogue from working.
      return null;
    }
  }

  static Future<List<Plant>> _loadBundledPlants() async {
    final rawJson = await rootBundle.loadString(_plantsAsset);
    final decoded = jsonDecode(rawJson);

    if (decoded is! Map<String, dynamic>) {
      throw const FormatException('plants.json must contain a JSON object.');
    }

    final rawPlants = decoded['plants'];
    if (rawPlants is! List) {
      throw const FormatException('plants.json must contain a "plants" array.');
    }

    final plants = <Plant>[];
    for (final item in rawPlants) {
      if (item is! Map) continue;
      final map = Map<String, dynamic>.from(item);
      try {
        plants.add(Plant.fromJson(map));
      } on Object catch (error) {
        throw FormatException('Invalid plant record in plants.json: $error');
      }
    }
    return plants;
  }

  /// Finds a plant by its stable ID.
  static Future<Plant?> getPlantById(String id) async {
    final plants = await loadPlants();
    for (final plant in plants) {
      if (plant.id == id) return plant;
    }
    return null;
  }

  /// Searches plant names, scientific names, and keywords.
  static Future<List<Plant>> searchPlants(String query) async {
    final plants = await loadPlants();
    final normalizedQuery = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (normalizedQuery.isEmpty) return List.unmodifiable(plants);

    return plants.where((plant) {
      final searchableText = [
        plant.commonName,
        plant.sanskritName ?? '',
        plant.hindiName ?? '',
        plant.scientificName,
        plant.family,
        plant.category,
        ...plant.keywords,
      ].join(' ').toLowerCase();
      return searchableText.contains(normalizedQuery);
    }).toList();
  }

  /// Clears the in-memory cache so the next load checks Firestore again.
  static void clearCache() {
    _plantsCache = null;
    _lastLoadUsedCloud = false;
  }
}

// Google AI Studio - Jeevan Sutra Indian Knowledge Library Screen
// Classical Vedic treatises bridged with modern clinical & scientific research
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../data/sample_data.dart';

class KnowledgeLibraryScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const KnowledgeLibraryScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<KnowledgeLibraryScreen> createState() => _KnowledgeLibraryScreenState();
}

class _KnowledgeLibraryScreenState extends State<KnowledgeLibraryScreen> {
  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();
  String _searchQuery = '';
  String _selectedTab = 'All';

  final List<String> _tabs = [
    'All',
    'Scientific Wellness',
    'Ayurveda & Traditional Wisdom',
    'Yoga & Meditation',
  ];

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _openArticleModal(KnowledgeArticle article) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (ctx) {
        final isDark = Theme.of(ctx).brightness == Brightness.dark;
        return DraggableScrollableSheet(
          initialChildSize: 0.85,
          minChildSize: 0.5,
          maxChildSize: 0.95,
          builder: (_, scrollController) {
            return Container(
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: isDark
                    ? JeevanSutraColors.darkSurface
                    : JeevanSutraColors.pureWhite,

                  top: Radius.circular(28),
                ),
              ),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 16, 20, 24),
              child: ListView(
                controller: scrollController,
                children: [
                  Center(
                    child: Container(
                      width: 40,

                        color: isDark
                            ? JeevanSutraColors.darkBorder
                            : JeevanSutraColors.subtleBorder,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
                      Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 10,
                          vertical: 4,
                        ),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: JeevanSutraColors.mutedGold.withValues(
                            alpha: 0.2,
                          ),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          article.category.toUpperCase(),
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 10,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            color: JeevanSutraColors.mutedGold,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      IconButton(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded),
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Text(
                    article.title,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 20,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: isDark
                          ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextPrimary
                          : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
                    ),
                  ),
                  Text(
                    article.subtitle,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 13,
                      fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                      color: isDark
                          ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                          : JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  Text(
                    "By ${article.author} â€¢ ${article.readingTimeMinutes} min read",
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 11.5,
                      color: isDark
                          ? JeevanSutraColors.freshLeaf
                          : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const Divider(height: 24),
                  Text(
                    article.content,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 14,
                      height: 1.6,
                      color: isDark
                          ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextPrimary
                          : JeevanSutraColors.mainText,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 20),
                  Wrap(
                    spacing: 8,
                    runSpacing: 8,
                    children: article.tags.map((t) {
                      return Chip(
                        label: Text("#$t"),
                        labelStyle: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11),
                        backgroundColor: isDark
                            ? JeevanSutraColors.darkCanvas
                            : JeevanSutraColors.warmIvory,
                      );
                    }).toList(),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;

    final filteredArticles = SampleData.knowledgeArticles.where((art) {
      final matchesTab = _selectedTab == 'All' || art.category == _selectedTab;
      final q = _searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      final matchesSearch =
          q.isEmpty ||
          art.title.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
          art.subtitle.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
          art.excerpt.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
          art.tags.any((t) => t.toLowerCase().contains(q));
      return matchesTab && matchesSearch;
    }).toList();

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text("Knowledge Library"),
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back_rounded),
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
        ),
      ),
      body: BotanicalBackground(
        child: SafeArea(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 16, 100),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                _buildHeader(isDark),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                _buildSearchBar(isDark),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildTabChips(isDark),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                _buildArticlesList(filteredArticles, isDark),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Header â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildHeader(bool isDark) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Row(
          children: [
            Icon(
              Icons.menu_book_rounded,
              size: 14,
              color: isDark
                  ? JeevanSutraColors.freshLeaf
                  : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            Text(
              "CLASSICAL TREATISES & SCIENCE",
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 10,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                letterSpacing: 1.1,
                color: isDark
                    ? JeevanSutraColors.freshLeaf
                    : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(
          "Indian Knowledge Library",
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 24,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            letterSpacing: -0.3,
            color: isDark
                ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextPrimary
                : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 2),
        Text(
          "Original translations from Charaka Samhita, Sushruta Samhita, and modern peer-reviewed integrative research.",
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            color: isDark
                ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                : JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Search Bar â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildSearchBar(bool isDark) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isDark
            ? JeevanSutraColors.darkSurface
            : JeevanSutraColors.pureWhite,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(
          color: isDark
              ? JeevanSutraColors.darkBorder
              : JeevanSutraColors.subtleBorder,
        ),
      ),
      child: TextField(
        controller: _searchController,
        onChanged: (val) => setState(() => _searchQuery = val),
        style: TextStyle(
          fontSize: 12.5,
          color: isDark
              ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextPrimary
              : JeevanSutraColors.mainText,
        ),
        decoration: InputDecoration(
          hintText:
              "Search Vedic treatises, chronobiology, Ojas, or breathwork...",
          hintStyle: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            color: isDark
                ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                : JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText,
          ),
          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search_rounded, size: 20),
          suffixIcon: _searchQuery.isNotEmpty
              ? IconButton(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded, size: 18),
                  onPressed: () {
                    _searchController.clear();
                    setState(() => _searchQuery = '');
                  },
                )
              : null,
          border: InputBorder.none,
          contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
            horizontal: 16,
            vertical: 12,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Tab Chips â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildTabChips(bool isDark) {
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      child: Row(
        children: _tabs.map((tab) {
          final isSelected = _selectedTab == tab;
          return Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 8),
            child: ChoiceChip(
              label: Text(tab),
              labelStyle: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 11,
                fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.w500,
                color: isSelected
                    ? Colors.white
                    : (isDark
                          ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                          : JeevanSutraColors.mainText),
              ),
              selected: isSelected,
              onSelected: (val) {
                if (val) setState(() => _selectedTab = tab);
              },
              backgroundColor: isDark
                  ? JeevanSutraColors.darkSurface
                  : JeevanSutraColors.pureWhite,
              selectedColor: isDark
                  ? JeevanSutraColors.freshLeaf
                  : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                side: BorderSide(
                  color: isSelected
                      ? Colors.transparent
                      : (isDark
                            ? JeevanSutraColors.darkBorder
                            : JeevanSutraColors.subtleBorder),
                ),
              ),
              showCheckmark: false,
            ),
          );
        }).toList(),
      ),
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Articles List â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildArticlesList(List<KnowledgeArticle> articles, bool isDark) {
    if (articles.isEmpty) {
      return Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Center(
          child: Text(
            "No articles match your search criteria.",
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 12,
              color: isDark
                  ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                  : JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    return ListView.separated(
      shrinkWrap: true,
      physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
      itemCount: articles.length,
      separatorBuilder: (_, _) => const SizedBox(height: 12),
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        final art = articles[index];

        return InkWell(
          onTap: () => _openArticleModal(art),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(18),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: isDark
                  ? JeevanSutraColors.darkSurface
                  : JeevanSutraColors.pureWhite,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
              border: Border.all(
                color: isDark
                    ? JeevanSutraColors.darkBorder
                    : JeevanSutraColors.subtleBorder,
              ),
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: isDark ? 0.2 : 0.03),
                  blurRadius: 8,
                  offset: const Offset(0, 2),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                        horizontal: 8,
                        vertical: 3,
                      ),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: JeevanSutraColors.mutedGold.withValues(
                          alpha: 0.2,
                        ),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        art.category.toUpperCase(),
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 9.5,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: JeevanSutraColors.mutedGold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        const Icon(
                          Icons.schedule_rounded,
                          size: 14,
                          color: JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 4),
                        Text(
                          "${art.readingTimeMinutes} min",
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 10.5,
                            color: isDark
                                ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                                : JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                Text(
                  art.title,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 15,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: isDark
                        ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextPrimary
                        : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
                  ),
                ),
                Text(
                  art.subtitle,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 11.5,
                    fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                    color: isDark
                        ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                        : JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                Text(
                  art.excerpt,
                  maxLines: 2,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 11.5,
                    color: isDark
                        ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                        : JeevanSutraColors.mainText,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      "By ${art.author}",
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 11,
                        color: JeevanSutraColors.mutedGold,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                      ),
                    ),
                    IconButton(
                      icon: Icon(
                        art.isBookmarked
                            ? Icons.bookmark_rounded
                            : Icons.bookmark_border_rounded,
                        color: art.isBookmarked
                            ? (isDark
                                  ? JeevanSutraColors.freshLeaf
                                  : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen)
                            : (isDark
                                  ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                                  : JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText),
                        size: 20,
                      ),
                      onPressed: () {
                        setState(() {
                          art.isBookmarked = !art.isBookmarked;
                        });
                        LocalStorageService.instance.saveWisdom(art.title);
                      },
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}


========== lib\screens\medicinal_plants_screen.dart ==========

// Google AI Studio - Jeevan Sutra Medicinal Plants Screen
// Classical Dravyaguna encyclopedia & phytochemical pharmacology
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../models/plant.dart';
import '../repositories/content_repository.dart';
import '../services/local_storage_service.dart';
import '../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../widgets/botanical_background.dart';
import 'plant_detail_screen.dart';

class MedicinalPlantsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const MedicinalPlantsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<MedicinalPlantsScreen> createState() => _MedicinalPlantsScreenState();
}

class _MedicinalPlantsScreenState extends State<MedicinalPlantsScreen> {
  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();
  List<Plant> _plants = [];
  Set<String> _savedPlants = {};
  bool _loading = true;

  String _searchQuery = '';
  String _selectedCategory = 'All';

  final List<String> _categories = [
    'All',
    'Adaptogen & Respiratory',
    'Neuro-Endocrine & Strength',
    'Anti-Inflammatory & Blood Purifier',
    'Nootropic & Cognitive Clarity',
  ];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadPlants();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _loadPlants() async {
    try {
      final results = await Future.wait([
        LocalStorageService.instance.getSavedPlants(),
        ContentRepository.loadPlants(),
        LocalStorageService.instance.getSavedPlantRecords(),
      ]);

      if (!mounted) return;
      final localPlants = List<Plant>.from(results[1] as List<Plant>);
      final savedRecords = List<Plant>.from(results[2] as List<Plant>);
      for (final record in savedRecords) {
        if (record.organismType != 'plant') continue;
        if (record.confidence != null && record.confidence! < 0.6) continue;
        final index = localPlants.indexWhere(
          (plant) =>
              plant.commonName.toLowerCase() ==
                  record.commonName.toLowerCase() ||
              plant.botanicalName.toLowerCase() ==
                  record.botanicalName.toLowerCase(),
        );
        if (index >= 0) {
          localPlants[index] = record;
        } else {
          localPlants.insert(0, record);
        }
      }
      setState(() {
        _savedPlants = Set<String>.from(results[0] as List<String>);
        _plants = localPlants;
        _loading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _loading = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _toggleBookmark(Plant plant) async {
    final name = plant.commonName;
    if (_savedPlants.contains(name)) {
      await LocalStorageService.instance.removePlant(name);
      setState(() => _savedPlants.remove(name));
    } else {
      await LocalStorageService.instance.savePlant(name);
      setState(() => _savedPlants.add(name));
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text("$name saved to your botanical sanctuary."),
            duration: const Duration(seconds: 1),
          ),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;

    final filteredPlants = _plants.where((p) {
      final q = _searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      final matchesSearch =
          q.isEmpty ||
          p.commonName.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
          (p.sanskritName?.toLowerCase().contains(q) ?? false) ||
          p.botanicalName.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
          (p.primaryUses?.toLowerCase().contains(q) ?? false);

      final matchesCategory =
          _selectedCategory == 'All' ||
          p.category.toLowerCase().contains(
            _selectedCategory.split(' ').first.toLowerCase(),
          );

      return matchesSearch && matchesCategory;
    }).toList();

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text("Medicinal Plants"),
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back_rounded),
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
        ),
      ),
      body: BotanicalBackground(
        child: SafeArea(
          child: _loading
              ? const Center(
                  child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                    color: JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
                  ),
                )
              : SingleChildScrollView(
                  physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 16, 100),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      _buildHeader(isDark),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      _buildSearchBar(isDark),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      _buildCategoryChips(isDark),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      _buildPlantGrid(filteredPlants, isDark),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Header â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildHeader(bool isDark) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Row(
          children: [
            Icon(
              Icons.eco_rounded,
              size: 14,
              color: isDark
                  ? JeevanSutraColors.freshLeaf
                  : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            Text(
              "VEDIC FLORA ENCYCLOPEDIA",
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 10,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                letterSpacing: 1.1,
                color: isDark
                    ? JeevanSutraColors.freshLeaf
                    : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(
          "Medicinal Plants & Dravyaguna",
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 24,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            letterSpacing: -0.3,
            color: isDark
                ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextPrimary
                : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 2),
        Text(
          "Phytochemical pharmacology, traditional virya, and therapeutic applications.",
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            color: isDark
                ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                : JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Search Bar â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildSearchBar(bool isDark) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isDark
            ? JeevanSutraColors.darkSurface
            : JeevanSutraColors.pureWhite,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(
          color: isDark
              ? JeevanSutraColors.darkBorder
              : JeevanSutraColors.subtleBorder,
        ),
      ),
      child: TextField(
        controller: _searchController,
        onChanged: (val) => setState(() => _searchQuery = val),
        style: TextStyle(
          fontSize: 12.5,
          color: isDark
              ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextPrimary
              : JeevanSutraColors.mainText,
        ),
        decoration: InputDecoration(
          hintText: "Search by common, Latin, or Sanskrit name...",
          hintStyle: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            color: isDark
                ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                : JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText,
          ),
          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search_rounded, size: 20),
          suffixIcon: _searchQuery.isNotEmpty
              ? IconButton(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded, size: 18),
                  onPressed: () {
                    _searchController.clear();
                    setState(() => _searchQuery = '');
                  },
                )
              : null,
          border: InputBorder.none,
          contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
            horizontal: 16,
            vertical: 12,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Category Filter Chips â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildCategoryChips(bool isDark) {
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      child: Row(
        children: _categories.map((cat) {
          final isSelected = _selectedCategory == cat;
          return Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 8),
            child: ChoiceChip(
              label: Text(cat),
              labelStyle: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 11,
                fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.w500,
                color: isSelected
                    ? Colors.white
                    : (isDark
                          ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                          : JeevanSutraColors.mainText),
              ),
              selected: isSelected,
              onSelected: (val) {
                if (val) setState(() => _selectedCategory = cat);
              },
              backgroundColor: isDark
                  ? JeevanSutraColors.darkSurface
                  : JeevanSutraColors.pureWhite,
              selectedColor: isDark
                  ? JeevanSutraColors.freshLeaf
                  : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                side: BorderSide(
                  color: isSelected
                      ? Colors.transparent
                      : (isDark
                            ? JeevanSutraColors.darkBorder
                            : JeevanSutraColors.subtleBorder),
                ),
              ),
              showCheckmark: false,
            ),
          );
        }).toList(),
      ),
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Plants List / Grid â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildPlantGrid(List<Plant> plants, bool isDark) {
    if (plants.isEmpty) {
      return Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Center(
          child: Text(
            "No botanical specimens found matching your query.",
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 12,
              color: isDark
                  ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                  : JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    return ListView.separated(
      shrinkWrap: true,
      physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
      itemCount: plants.length,
      separatorBuilder: (_, _) => const SizedBox(height: 12),
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        final plant = plants[index];
        final isSaved = _savedPlants.contains(plant.commonName);

        return InkWell(
          onTap: () {
            Navigator.push(
              context,
              MaterialPageRoute(
                builder: (_) => PlantDetailScreen(plant: plant),
              ),
            );
          },
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: isDark
                  ? JeevanSutraColors.darkSurface
                  : JeevanSutraColors.pureWhite,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
              border: Border.all(
                color: isDark
                    ? JeevanSutraColors.darkBorder
                    : JeevanSutraColors.subtleBorder,
              ),
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: isDark ? 0.2 : 0.03),
                  blurRadius: 8,
                  offset: const Offset(0, 2),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            child: Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Container(
                  width: 44,
                  height: 44,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: isDark
                        ? JeevanSutraColors.darkCanvas
                        : JeevanSutraColors.softSage,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                  ),
                  child: const Icon(
                    Icons.eco_rounded,
                    color: JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
                    size: 22,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 14),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                        children: [
                          Expanded(
                            child: Text(
                              plant.commonName,
                              style: TextStyle(
                                fontSize: 15,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                color: isDark
                                    ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextPrimary
                                    : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          if (plant.doshaImpact != null)
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                horizontal: 7,
                                vertical: 2,
                              ),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: isDark
                                    ? JeevanSutraColors.darkCanvas
                                    : JeevanSutraColors.warmIvory,
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                              ),
                              child: Text(
                                plant.doshaImpact!,
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontSize: 9,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  color: JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      Text(
                        "${plant.sanskritName != null ? '${plant.sanskritName} â€¢ ' : ''}${plant.botanicalName}",
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11,
                          fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                          color: isDark
                              ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                              : JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText,
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (plant.primaryUses != null) ...[
                        const SizedBox(height: 6),
                        Text(
                          plant.primaryUses!,
                          maxLines: 2,
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 11.5,
                            color: isDark
                                ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                                : JeevanSutraColors.mainText,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                IconButton(
                  icon: Icon(
                    isSaved
                        ? Icons.bookmark_rounded
                        : Icons.bookmark_border_rounded,
                    color: isSaved
                        ? (isDark
                              ? JeevanSutraColors.freshLeaf
                              : JeevanSutraColors.forestGreen)
                        : (isDark
                              ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
                              : JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText),
                    size: 20,
                  ),
                  onPressed: () => _toggleBookmark(plant),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}


========== lib\screens\plant_detail_screen.dart ==========

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../models/plant.dart';
import '../models/wellness_models.dart' show MedicinalPlant;
import '../services/local_storage_service.dart';
import '../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../widgets/botanical_background.dart';

class PlantDetailScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final Plant plant;

  const PlantDetailScreen({super.key, required this.plant});

  /// Convenience constructor from a [MedicinalPlant] (wellness_models).
  factory PlantDetailScreen.fromMedicinalPlant(MedicinalPlant mp, {Key? key}) {
    final plant = Plant(
      id: mp.id,
      commonName: mp.commonName,
      sanskritName: mp.sanskritName,
      scientificName: mp.botanicalName,
      family: mp.category,
      category: mp.category,
      identification: mp.primaryUses,
      traditionalUses: [mp.traditionalApplication],
      scientificEvidence: [mp.scientificEvidence],
      safetySummary: mp.precautions,
      precautions: [mp.precautions],
      knownCompounds: [
        '${mp.rasa} (rasa) â€¢ ${mp.virya} (virya) â€¢ ${mp.vipaka} (vipaka)',
      ],
    );
    return PlantDetailScreen(key: key, plant: plant);
  }

  @override
  State<PlantDetailScreen> createState() => _PlantDetailScreenState();
}

class _PlantDetailScreenState extends State<PlantDetailScreen>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  final LocalStorageService _storage = LocalStorageService.instance;

  late final TabController _tabController;
  bool _saved = false;
  bool _loadingSavedState = true;

  Plant get plant => widget.plant;
  bool get _isUnverifiedAiScan => plant.reviewStatus == 'ai_scan_unverified';

  final List<String> _tabs = const [
    'Overview',
    'Benefits',
    'Uses',
    'Precautions',
  ];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tabController = TabController(length: _tabs.length, vsync: this);
    _loadSavedState();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tabController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _loadSavedState() async {
    final savedPlants = await _storage.getSavedPlants();

    if (!mounted) return;

    setState(() {
      _saved = savedPlants.contains(plant.commonName);
      _loadingSavedState = false;
    });
  }

  Future<void> _toggleSaved() async {
    await _storage.toggleSavedPlant(plant.commonName);

    if (!mounted) return;

    setState(() {
      _saved = !_saved;
    });

    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
      ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
      ..showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 1300),
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
          content: Text(
            _saved
                ? '${plant.commonName} saved to My Herbs'
                : '${plant.commonName} removed from My Herbs',
          ),
        ),
      );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
      body: BotanicalBackground(
        child: SafeArea(
          bottom: false,
          child: Column(
            children: [
              _buildTopBar(context),
              Expanded(
                child: NestedScrollView(
                  headerSliverBuilder: (context, innerBoxIsScrolled) {
                    return [
                      SliverToBoxAdapter(
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 6, 20, 16),
                          child: _buildHeroCard(),
                        ),
                      ),
                      SliverPersistentHeader(
                        pinned: true,
                        delegate: _TabBarHeaderDelegate(
                          tabBar: _buildCustomTabBar(),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ];
                  },
                  body: TabBarView(
                    controller: _tabController,
                    children: [
                      _buildOverviewTab(),
                      _buildBenefitsTab(),
                      _buildUsesTab(),
                      _buildPrecautionsTab(),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              _buildBottomCta(),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Top Bar â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildTopBar(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          _CircleIconButton(
            icon: Icons.arrow_back_rounded,
            onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          ),
          Text(
            plant.commonName,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: context.detailText,
              fontSize: 17,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
            ),
          ),
          _CircleIconButton(
            icon: _saved
                ? Icons.bookmark_rounded
                : Icons.bookmark_border_rounded,
            iconColor: _saved ? AppTheme.gold : context.detailSecondaryText,
            onTap: _loadingSavedState ? null : _toggleSaved,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Hero Card â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildHeroCard() {
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: const LinearGradient(
          colors: [Color(0xFF0E281C), Color(0xFF183D2A), Color(0xFF24573C)],
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(28),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.borderGold),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: AppTheme.emerald.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
            blurRadius: 26,
            offset: const Offset(0, 10),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Stack(
        children: [
          Positioned(
            right: -30,
            top: -30,
            child: Container(
              width: 140,
              height: 140,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                gradient: RadialGradient(
                  colors: [
                    AppTheme.gold.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                    Colors.transparent,
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Positioned(
            right: 14,
            bottom: 10,
            child: Icon(
              Icons.spa_rounded,
              size: 110,
              color: AppTheme.gold.withValues(alpha: 0.08),
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(22),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      width: 58,
                      height: 58,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        gradient: AppTheme.goldGradient,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                        boxShadow: [
                          BoxShadow(
                            color: AppTheme.gold.withValues(alpha: 0.35),
                            blurRadius: 14,
                            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      child: Icon(
                        _plantIcon,
                        size: 30,
                        color: const Color(0xFF141004),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 14),
                    Expanded(

                        children: [
                          Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              horizontal: 10,
                              vertical: 4,
                            ),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: AppTheme.gold.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                              border: Border.all(
                                color: AppTheme.gold.withValues(alpha: 0.35),
                              ),
                            ),
                            child: Text(
                              plant.category.toUpperCase(),
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                color: AppTheme.goldLight,
                                fontSize: 9.5,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                                letterSpacing: 1.2,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 6),
                          Text(
                            plant.commonName,
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              color: Colors.white,
                              fontSize: 24,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                              height: 1.1,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 3),
                          Text(
                            plant.scientificName,
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              color: AppTheme.goldLight,
                              fontSize: 12.5,
                              fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                if (plant.sanskritName != null || plant.hindiName != null) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  Wrap(
                    spacing: 8,
                    runSpacing: 6,
                    children: [
                      if (plant.sanskritName != null)
                        _HeroTagPill(label: 'Sanskrit: ${plant.sanskritName}'),
                      if (plant.hindiName != null)
                        _HeroTagPill(label: 'Hindi: ${plant.hindiName}'),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Custom Tab Bar â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildCustomTabBar() {
    return Container(
      color: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 8),
      child: Container(
        height: 44,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
          border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.borderGold),
        ),
        child: TabBar(
          controller: _tabController,
          indicator: BoxDecoration(
            gradient: AppTheme.goldGradient,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(
                color: AppTheme.gold.withValues(alpha: 0.35),
                blurRadius: 10,
                offset: const Offset(0, 3),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          indicatorSize: TabBarIndicatorSize.tab,
          dividerColor: Colors.transparent,
          labelColor: const Color(0xFF141004),
          unselectedLabelColor: context.detailSecondaryText,
          labelStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
          ),
          unselectedLabelStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
          tabs: _tabs.map((title) => Tab(text: title)).toList(),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Tab 1: Overview â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildOverviewTab() {
    return ListView(
      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 14, 20, 24),
      children: [
        _DarkCard(
          title: 'Plant Profile',
          icon: Icons.local_florist_rounded,
          child: Column(
            children: [
              _DarkInfoRow(
                label: 'Scientific Name',
                value: plant.scientificName,
                italic: true,
              ),
              if (plant.family.isNotEmpty)
                _DarkInfoRow(label: 'Botanical Family', value: plant.family),
              if (plant.category.isNotEmpty)
                _DarkInfoRow(label: 'Ayurvedic Class', value: plant.category),
              if (plant.partsUsed.isNotEmpty)
                _DarkListPills(
                  title: 'Parts Traditionally Used',
                  items: plant.partsUsed,
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        if (plant.identification.isNotEmpty) ...[
          _DarkCard(
            title: 'Botanical Identification',
            icon: Icons.visibility_rounded,
            child: Text(
              plant.identification,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: context.detailSecondaryText,
                fontSize: 13,
                height: 1.5,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
        ],
        _DarkCard(
          title: 'Evidence Verification',
          icon: Icons.verified_outlined,
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: 10,
                  vertical: 6,
                ),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppTheme.emerald.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  border: Border.all(
                    color: AppTheme.emerald.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
                  ),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  _formatStatus(plant.evidenceLevel),
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: AppTheme.emeraldLight,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                    fontSize: 11,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const Spacer(),
              Text(
                'Status: ${_formatStatus(plant.reviewStatus)}',
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: context.detailSecondaryText,
                  fontSize: 11.5,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _buildDisclaimerBanner(),
      ],
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Tab 2: Benefits â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildBenefitsTab() {
    return ListView(
      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 14, 20, 24),
      children: [
        _DarkCard(
          title: _isUnverifiedAiScan
              ? 'AI-Reported Evidence (Unverified)'
              : 'Researched Benefits & Evidence',
          icon: Icons.science_rounded,
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              if (_isUnverifiedAiScan) ...[
                Text(
                  'This AI-generated summary has not been independently verified and is not medical advice.',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: context.detailSecondaryText,
                    fontSize: 12,
                    height: 1.4,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
              ],
              if (plant.scientificEvidence.isEmpty)
                Text(
                  'Scientific research data is being verified for this record.',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: context.detailSecondaryText,
                    fontSize: 13,
                  ),
                )
              else
                ...plant.scientificEvidence.map(
                  (item) => Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
                    child: Row(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        const Padding(
                          padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
                          child: Icon(
                            Icons.circle,
                            size: 6,
                            color: AppTheme.gold,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 10),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Text(
                            item,
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: context.detailSecondaryText,
                              fontSize: 13,
                              height: 1.45,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        if (plant.knownCompounds.isNotEmpty) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          _DarkCard(
            title: 'Key Active Phytochemicals',
            icon: Icons.biotech_rounded,
            child: Wrap(
              spacing: 8,
              runSpacing: 8,
              children: plant.knownCompounds.map((compound) {
                return Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                    horizontal: 12,
                    vertical: 6,
                  ),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: context.detailInsetSurface,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                    border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.borderGoldSubtle),
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    compound,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      color: AppTheme.goldLight,
                      fontSize: 11.5,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              }).toList(),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Tab 3: Uses â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildUsesTab() {
    return ListView(
      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 14, 20, 24),
      children: [
        _DarkCard(
          title: _isUnverifiedAiScan
              ? 'AI-Reported Traditional Uses (Unverified)'
              : 'Traditional Ayurvedic Uses',
          icon: Icons.history_edu_rounded,
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              if (_isUnverifiedAiScan) ...[
                Text(
                  'Traditional descriptions are unverified and do not establish safety or effectiveness. Do not consume or use based only on this scan.',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: context.detailSecondaryText,
                    fontSize: 12,
                    height: 1.4,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
              ],
              if (plant.traditionalUses.isEmpty)
                Text(
                  'Traditional applications are being curated from classical texts.',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: context.detailSecondaryText,
                    fontSize: 13,
                  ),
                )
              else
                ...plant.traditionalUses.map(
                  (use) => Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                    child: Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: context.detailInsetSurface,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.borderGoldSubtle),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          const Icon(
                            Icons.spa_rounded,
                            color: AppTheme.gold,
                            size: 18,
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 10),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Text(
                              use,
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: context.detailText,
                                fontSize: 13,
                                height: 1.45,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        if (plant.traditionalReferences.isNotEmpty) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          _DarkCard(
            title: 'Classical Texts & References',
            icon: Icons.menu_book_rounded,
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: plant.traditionalReferences.map((ref) {
                return Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
                  child: Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      const Icon(
                        Icons.bookmark_border_rounded,
                        color: AppTheme.goldLight,
                        size: 16,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          ref,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: context.detailSecondaryText,
                            fontSize: 12,
                            height: 1.4,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                );
              }).toList(),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Tab 4: Precautions â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildPrecautionsTab() {
    return ListView(
      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 14, 20, 24),
      children: [
        if (plant.isToxic == true || plant.toxicityWarning != null) ...[
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0xFF2E1515),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
              border: Border.all(
                color: const Color(0xFFE57373).withValues(alpha: 0.4),
              ),
            ),
            child: Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                const Icon(
                  Icons.warning_amber_rounded,
                  color: Color(0xFFE57373),
                  size: 24,
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    plant.toxicityWarning ?? 'Caution: This botanical specimen contains potent compounds. Do not ingest without medical supervision.',
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      color: Color(0xFFFFCDD2),
                      fontSize: 13,
                      height: 1.45,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
        ],
        _DarkCard(
          title: 'Safety Guidelines & Precautions',
          icon: Icons.shield_outlined,
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              if (plant.safetySummary.isNotEmpty) ...[
                Text(
                  plant.safetySummary,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: context.detailSecondaryText,
                    fontSize: 13,
                    height: 1.5,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 14),
              ],
              if (plant.precautions.isNotEmpty)
                ...plant.precautions.map(
                  (precaution) => Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
                    child: Row(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        const Icon(
                          Icons.check_circle_outline_rounded,
                          size: 16,
                          color: AppTheme.gold,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Text(
                            precaution,
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: context.detailSecondaryText,
                              fontSize: 12.5,
                              height: 1.4,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                )
              else
                Text(
                  'No adverse interaction precautions currently recorded.',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: context.detailSecondaryText,
                    fontSize: 13,
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Bottom CTA: Add to My Herbs â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildBottomCta() {

                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
              ),
            ),

                  ? Icons.bookmark_added_rounded
                  : Icons.bookmark_add_rounded,
              color: _saved ? AppTheme.gold : const Color(0xFF141004),

            ),

              _saved ? 'Added to My Herbs' : 'Add to My Herbs',

      ),

  // â”€â”€â”€ Helpers â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  Widget _buildDisclaimerBanner() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.borderGoldSubtle),
      ),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Icon(
            Icons.info_outline_rounded,
            color: AppTheme.gold,
            size: 20,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              'Jeevan Sutra provides educational Indian Knowledge information and does not replace medical advice or prescription.',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 11.5,
                height: 1.45,
                color: context.detailSecondaryText,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  IconData get _plantIcon {
    switch (plant.id) {
      case 'tulsi':
        return Icons.eco_rounded;
      case 'neem':
        return Icons.spa_rounded;
      case 'aloe_vera':
        return Icons.grass_rounded;
      case 'ashwagandha':
        return Icons.local_florist_rounded;
      case 'turmeric':
        return Icons.grass_rounded;
      case 'dhatura':
        return Icons.warning_amber_rounded;
      default:
        return Icons.local_florist_rounded;
    }
  }

  String _formatStatus(String status) {
    if (status.trim().isEmpty) return 'General';
    return status
        .replaceAll('_', ' ')
        .split(' ')
        .map((w) => w.isEmpty ? w : '${w[0].toUpperCase()}${w.substring(1)}')
        .join(' ');
  }
}
