// Persistent Header Delegate for Pinned TabBar
// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

class _TabBarHeaderDelegate extends SliverPersistentHeaderDelegate {
  final Widget tabBar;

  _TabBarHeaderDelegate({required this.tabBar});

  @override
  double get minExtent => 60;
  @override
  double get maxExtent => 60;

  @override
  Widget build(
    BuildContext context,
    double shrinkOffset,
    bool overlapsContent,
  ) {
    return tabBar;
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRebuild(covariant _TabBarHeaderDelegate oldDelegate) {
    return oldDelegate.tabBar != tabBar;
  }
}

// Resolve legacy detail colors through the active botanical theme.
extension _PlantDetailColors on BuildContext {
  Color get detailText => Theme.of(this).colorScheme.onSurface;

  Color get detailSecondaryText => Theme.of(this).brightness == Brightness.dark
      ? JeevanSutraColors.darkTextSecondary
      : JeevanSutraColors.secondaryText;

  Color get detailInsetSurface => Theme.of(this).brightness == Brightness.dark
      ? JeevanSutraColors.darkSurfaceElevated
      : JeevanSutraColors.softSage;
}


========== lib\services\gemini_plant_service.dart ==========

import 'dart:typed_data';

import '../models/plant.dart';
import 'gemini_service.dart';

class GeminiPlantService {
  GeminiPlantService({Future<PlantScanResult> Function(Uint8List)? analyzer})
    : _analyzer = analyzer ?? GeminiService.instance.analyzePlantImage;

  static final GeminiPlantService instance = GeminiPlantService();
  final Future<PlantScanResult> Function(Uint8List) _analyzer;

  Future<Plant?> identifyPlant(Uint8List imageBytes) async {
    final result = await _analyzer(imageBytes);
    if (result.organismType != 'plant' || result.confidence < 0.6) return null;

    return Plant(
      id: 'ai_scan',
      commonName: result.plantName,
      sanskritName: result.sanskritName,
      hindiName: result.hindiName,
      scientificName: result.scientificName ?? result.plantName,
      family: result.family ?? '',
      category: result.plantCategory ?? 'AI identified (unverified)',
      medicinalClassification: result.medicinalClassification,
      growingInformation: result.growingInformation,
      identification: result.identification,
      traditionalUses: [result.traditionalUses],
      scientificEvidence: [result.scientificEvidence],
      safetySummary: result.safetySummary,
      precautions: result.precautions,
      isToxic: result.isToxic,
      toxicityWarning: result.toxicityWarning,
      organismType: result.organismType,
      confidence: result.confidence,
      reviewStatus: 'ai_scan_unverified',
    );
  }
}


========== lib\services\gemini_service.dart ==========

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:firebase_app_check/firebase_app_check.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

import '../models/plant.dart';
import '../repositories/content_repository.dart';

class PlantScanResult {
  final String plantName;
  final String? sanskritName;
  final String? hindiName;
  final String? scientificName;
  final String? family;
  final String? plantCategory;
  final String? medicinalClassification;
  final String? growingInformation;
  final String organismType;
  final double confidence;
  final bool liveAiAvailable;
  final String identification;
  final String traditionalUses;
  final String scientificEvidence;
  final String safetySummary;
  final List<String> precautions;
  final bool? isToxic;
  final String? toxicityWarning;
  final Plant? matchedLocalPlant;

  const PlantScanResult({
    required this.plantName,
    this.sanskritName,
    this.hindiName,
    this.scientificName,
    this.family,
    this.plantCategory,
    this.medicinalClassification,
    this.growingInformation,
    this.organismType = 'plant',
    required this.confidence,
    this.liveAiAvailable = true,
    required this.identification,
    required this.traditionalUses,
    required this.scientificEvidence,
    required this.safetySummary,
    this.precautions = const [],
    this.isToxic,
    this.toxicityWarning,
    this.matchedLocalPlant,
  });
}

class GeminiBackendException implements Exception {
  const GeminiBackendException(this.code);

  final String code;

  @override
  String toString() => code;
}

class GeminiService {
  GeminiService._({
    http.Client? httpClient,
    String? backendBaseUrl,
    this._appCheckTokenProvider,
    this._plantLoader,
  }) : _httpClient = httpClient ?? http.Client(),
       _backendBaseUrl = backendBaseUrl ?? _defaultBackendBaseUrl;

  static final GeminiService instance = GeminiService._();

  static const String modelName = 'gemini-3.8-flash';
  static const String _defaultBackendBaseUrl = String.fromEnvironment(
    'GEMINI_BACKEND_BASE_URL',
    defaultValue: 'https://jeevan-sutra-gemini-api.jeevan-sutra-ai.workers.dev',
  );

  static const _requestTimeout = Duration(seconds: 60);
  final http.Client _httpClient;
  final String _backendBaseUrl;
  final Future<String?> Function()? _appCheckTokenProvider;
  final Future<List<Plant>> Function()? _plantLoader;

  @visibleForTesting
  GeminiService.forTesting({
    required http.Client httpClient,
    required String backendBaseUrl,
    required Future<String?> Function() appCheckTokenProvider,
    Future<List<Plant>> Function()? plantLoader,
  }) : this._(
         httpClient: httpClient,
         backendBaseUrl: backendBaseUrl,
         appCheckTokenProvider: appCheckTokenProvider,
         plantLoader: plantLoader,
       );

  Future<String?> getApiKey() async {
    return null;
  }

  Future<bool> hasApiKey() async {
    return _backendBaseUrl.isNotEmpty;
  }

  @visibleForTesting
  static List<Map<String, String>> buildChatHistory(
    List<Map<String, dynamic>> conversationHistory,
  ) {
    final messages = <Map<String, String>>[];
    for (final message in conversationHistory) {
      final text = (message['text'] ?? '').toString().trim();
      if (text.isEmpty) continue;
      final role =
          message['role'] ?? (message['isUser'] == true ? 'user' : 'model');
      if (role == 'user') {
        messages.add({'role': 'user', 'text': text});
      } else if (role == 'model') {
        messages.add({'role': 'model', 'text': text});
      }
    }
    final recent = messages.length > 10
        ? messages.sublist(messages.length - 10)
        : messages;
    // Truncating a long conversation can leave an orphaned model turn.
    while (recent.isNotEmpty && recent.first['role'] != 'user') {
      recent.removeAt(0);
    }
    return recent;
  }

  Future<Map<String, String>> _requestHeaders() async {
    try {
      final token =
          await (_appCheckTokenProvider?.call() ??
              FirebaseAppCheck.instance.getToken());
      debugPrint('[AI_HEADERS] Token retrieved: ${token != null && token.isNotEmpty}');
      if (token == null || token.isEmpty) {
        throw const GeminiBackendException('APP_CHECK_UNAVAILABLE');
      }
      return {'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Firebase-AppCheck': token};
    } on GeminiBackendException {
      rethrow;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('[AI_HEADERS] Token exception: $e');
      throw const GeminiBackendException('APP_CHECK_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
  }

  Uri _endpoint(String name) {
    final base = Uri.tryParse(_backendBaseUrl);
    if (base == null ||
        base.scheme != 'https' ||
        base.host.isEmpty ||
        base.userInfo.isNotEmpty) {
      throw const GeminiBackendException('BACKEND_NOT_CONFIGURED');
    }
    return base.resolve('/$name');
  }

  Future<GeminiBackendException> _responseError(
    http.StreamedResponse response,
  ) async {
    if (response.statusCode == 403 || response.statusCode == 401) {
      return const GeminiBackendException('APP_CHECK_REJECTED');
    }
    if (response.statusCode == 429) {
      return const GeminiBackendException('RATE_LIMITED');
    }
    try {
      final body = await response.stream
          .transform(utf8.decoder)
          .join()
          .timeout(_requestTimeout);
      final decoded = jsonDecode(body);
      if (decoded is Map<String, dynamic> && decoded['error'] is String) {
        return GeminiBackendException(decoded['error'] as String);
      }
    } catch (_) {}
    return GeminiBackendException(
      response.statusCode >= 500 ? 'BACKEND_UNAVAILABLE' : 'INVALID_RESPONSE',
    );
  }

  GeminiBackendException _jsonResponseError(http.Response response) {
    if (response.statusCode == 403 || response.statusCode == 401) {
      return const GeminiBackendException('APP_CHECK_REJECTED');
    }
    if (response.statusCode == 429) {
      return const GeminiBackendException('RATE_LIMITED');
    }
    try {
      final decoded = jsonDecode(response.body);
      if (decoded is Map<String, dynamic> && decoded['error'] is String) {
        return GeminiBackendException(decoded['error'] as String);
      }
    } catch (_) {}
    return GeminiBackendException(
      response.statusCode >= 500 ? 'BACKEND_UNAVAILABLE' : 'INVALID_RESPONSE',
    );
  }

  @visibleForTesting
  static Map<String, dynamic> decodePlantResponse(String response) {
    final clean = response
        .trim()
        .replaceFirst(RegExp(r'^```(?:json)?\s*', caseSensitive: false), '')
        .replaceFirst(RegExp(r'\s*```$'), '');
    final decoded = jsonDecode(clean);
    if (decoded is! Map<String, dynamic>) {
      throw const FormatException('Plant analysis must be a JSON object.');
    }
    if (decoded['plantName'] is! String) {
      throw const FormatException('Plant analysis is missing its plant name.');
    }
    return decoded;
  }

  static String imageMimeType(Uint8List bytes) {
    bool startsWith(List<int> signature) {
      if (bytes.length < signature.length) return false;
      for (var index = 0; index < signature.length; index++) {
        if (bytes[index] != signature[index]) return false;
      }
      return true;
    }

    if (startsWith([0xff, 0xd8, 0xff])) return 'image/jpeg';
    if (startsWith([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a])) {
      return 'image/png';
    }
    if (bytes.length >= 12 &&
        ascii.decode(bytes.sublist(0, 4), allowInvalid: true) == 'RIFF' &&
        ascii.decode(bytes.sublist(8, 12), allowInvalid: true) == 'WEBP') {
      return 'image/webp';
    }
    if (bytes.length >= 12 &&
        ascii.decode(bytes.sublist(4, 8), allowInvalid: true) == 'ftyp') {
      final brand = ascii.decode(bytes.sublist(8, 12), allowInvalid: true);
      if (['heic', 'heix', 'hevc', 'hevx'].contains(brand)) return 'image/heic';
      if (['mif1', 'msf1', 'heif'].contains(brand)) return 'image/heif';
    }
    throw const FormatException(
      'Choose a JPEG, PNG, WebP, or HEIF plant photo.',
    );
  }

  static String describeFailure(Object error) {
    if (error is TimeoutException) {
      return 'The AI service took too long to respond. Please retry.';
    }
    if (error is GeminiBackendException) {
      switch (error.code) {
        case 'APP_CHECK_UNAVAILABLE':
        case 'APP_CHECK_REJECTED':
          return 'AI access could not be verified. Please try again later.';
        case 'BACKEND_NOT_CONFIGURED':
          return 'The AI service address is not configured for this app.';
        case 'AI_NOT_CONFIGURED':
          return 'The AI service is not configured. Please contact support.';
        case 'RATE_LIMITED':
        case 'AI_RATE_LIMITED':
          return 'The AI service is busy. Please wait a moment and try again.';
        case 'INVALID_REQUEST':
          return 'The request could not be processed. Please try again.';
        case 'INVALID_RESPONSE':
        case 'INVALID_AI_RESPONSE':
        case 'EMPTY_AI_RESPONSE':
          return 'The AI service returned an incomplete response. Please retry.';
        default:
          return 'The AI service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again later.';
      }
    }
    if (error is FormatException) {
      return error.message.startsWith('Choose a ')
          ? error.message
          : 'The AI service returned an incomplete plant analysis. Please retry with a clear photo.';
    }
    return 'Please check your connection and try again.';
  }

  Stream<String> streamChatResponse({
    required String prompt,
    required List<Map<String, dynamic>> conversationHistory,
  }) async* {
    final request = http.Request('POST', _endpoint('geminiChat'))
      ..headers.addAll(await _requestHeaders())
      ..body = jsonEncode({
        'prompt': prompt,
        'history': buildChatHistory(conversationHistory),
      });
    final response = await _httpClient.send(request).timeout(_requestTimeout);
    if (response.statusCode != 200) {
      throw await _responseError(response);
    }
    if (response.headers['content-type']?.contains('text/event-stream') !=
        true) {
      throw const GeminiBackendException('INVALID_RESPONSE');
    }

    var receivedText = false;
    await for (final line
        in response.stream
            .transform(utf8.decoder)
            .transform(const LineSplitter())
            .timeout(_requestTimeout)) {
      if (!line.startsWith('data:')) continue;
      final data = line.substring(5).trim();
      if (data == '[DONE]') break;
      final decoded = jsonDecode(data);
      if (decoded is! Map<String, dynamic>) {
        throw const GeminiBackendException('INVALID_RESPONSE');
      }
      if (decoded['error'] is String) {
        throw GeminiBackendException(decoded['error'] as String);
      }
      final text = decoded['text'];
      if (text is String && text.isNotEmpty) {
        receivedText = true;
        yield text;
      }
    }
    if (!receivedText) {
      throw const GeminiBackendException('EMPTY_AI_RESPONSE');
    }
  }

  Future<PlantScanResult> analyzePlantImage(Uint8List imageBytes) async {
    try {
      final endpoint = _endpoint('geminiPlant');
      final headers = await _requestHeaders();
      debugPrint('[AI_CAMERA] POST $endpoint');
      final response = await _httpClient
          .post(
            endpoint,
            headers: headers,
            body: jsonEncode({
              'mimeType': imageMimeType(imageBytes),
              'imageBase64': base64Encode(imageBytes),
            }),
          )
          .timeout(_requestTimeout);
      debugPrint('[AI_CAMERA] Response code: ${response.statusCode}');
      debugPrint('[AI_CAMERA] Response body: ${response.body}');
      if (response.statusCode != 200) {
        throw _jsonResponseError(response);
      }

      final decoded = jsonDecode(response.body);
      if (decoded is! Map<String, dynamic>) {
        throw const FormatException('Plant analysis must be a JSON object.');
      }
      final result = parsePlantScanResult(decoded);
      Plant? matched;
      if (result.organismType == 'plant') {
        try {
          matched = await _findMatchingLocalPlant(
            plantName: result.plantName,
            scientificName: result.scientificName,
            sanskritName: result.sanskritName,
          );
        } catch (_) {
          // A local content lookup must never discard a successful AI result.
        }
      }
      return matched == null
          ? result
          : parsePlantScanResult(decoded, matchedLocalPlant: matched);
    } catch (error, stack) {
      debugPrint('[AI_CAMERA] analyzePlantImage caught error: $error\n$stack');
      return _unavailablePlantResult(describeFailure(error));
    }
  }

  static PlantScanResult parsePlantScanResult(
    Map<String, dynamic> data, {
    Plant? matchedLocalPlant,
  }) {
    String? stringField(String key) {
      final value = data[key];
      return value is String && value.trim().isNotEmpty ? value.trim() : null;
    }

    final rawConfidence = data['confidence'];
    final parsedConfidence = rawConfidence is num
        ? rawConfidence.toDouble()
        : double.tryParse(rawConfidence?.toString() ?? '') ?? 0.0;
    final confidence =
        parsedConfidence.isFinite &&
            parsedConfidence >= 0.0 &&
            parsedConfidence <= 1.0
        ? parsedConfidence
        : 0.0;

    const organismTypes = ['plant', 'fungus', 'non-plant', 'uncertain'];
    final rawOrganismType = data['organismType'];
    final reportedType =
        rawOrganismType is String && organismTypes.contains(rawOrganismType)
        ? rawOrganismType
        : 'uncertain';
    final organismType = confidence < 0.6 ? 'uncertain' : reportedType;
    final isPlant = organismType == 'plant';
    const medicinalClassifications = ['medicinal', 'non-medicinal', 'unknown'];
    final rawMedicinalClassification = stringField('medicinalClassification');
    final isToxic = data['isToxic'] is bool ? data['isToxic'] as bool : null;
    final rawToxicityWarning = stringField('toxicityWarning');
    final toxicityWarning =
        rawToxicityWarning ??
        (isToxic == true
            ? 'Potential toxicity was reported. Do not touch or consume this organism.'
            : null);
    final rawPrecautions = data['precautions'];
    final precautions = isPlant && rawPrecautions is List
        ? rawPrecautions.whereType<String>().toList()
        : <String>[];
    precautions.add(
      isPlant
          ? 'Image identification cannot establish safety or edibility. Do not consume based on this result.'
          : 'Do not consume or use an unidentified organism medicinally.',
    );

    final rawName = stringField('plantName');
    final plantName = switch (organismType) {
      'fungus' => 'Fungus detected (not a plant)',
      'non-plant' => 'No plant detected',
      _ => rawName ?? 'Unidentified Botanical',
    };

    return PlantScanResult(
      plantName: plantName,
      sanskritName: isPlant ? stringField('sanskritName') : null,
      hindiName: isPlant ? stringField('hindiName') : null,
      scientificName: isPlant ? stringField('scientificName') : null,
      family: isPlant ? stringField('family') : null,
      plantCategory: isPlant ? stringField('plantCategory') : null,
      medicinalClassification:
          isPlant &&
              medicinalClassifications.contains(rawMedicinalClassification)
          ? rawMedicinalClassification
          : null,
      growingInformation: isPlant ? stringField('growingInformation') : null,
      organismType: organismType,
      confidence: confidence,
      identification: isPlant
          ? stringField('identification') ??
                'Visible plant characteristics were not provided.'
          : switch (organismType) {
              'fungus' => 'A fungus was identified, not a plant.',
              'non-plant' => 'No plant was identified in this image.',
              _ =>
                'The image does not support a reliable plant identification.',
            },
      traditionalUses: isPlant
          ? stringField('traditionalUses') ??
                'Traditional uses were not confidently identified.'
          : 'Not shown because this image is not confidently identified as a plant.',
      scientificEvidence: isPlant
          ? stringField('scientificEvidence') ??
                'No specific scientific evidence was confidently identified.'
          : 'Not shown because this image is not confidently identified as a plant.',
      safetySummary: isPlant
          ? 'Image identification cannot establish safety or edibility. Do not consume or use medicinally based only on this result.'
          : 'This image is not confirmed as a plant. Do not consume or use it medicinally.',
      precautions: precautions,
      isToxic: isToxic,
      toxicityWarning: toxicityWarning,
      matchedLocalPlant: matchedLocalPlant,
    );
  }

  PlantScanResult _unavailablePlantResult([String? reason]) => PlantScanResult(
    plantName: 'Unable to identify plant',
    organismType: 'uncertain',
    confidence: 0.0,
    liveAiAvailable: false,
    identification: reason ?? 'AI analysis could not complete. Retry with a clear photo of one plant.',
    traditionalUses:
        'Not shown because the plant could not be identified reliably.',
    scientificEvidence:
        'Not shown because the plant could not be identified reliably.',
    safetySummary: 'Do not consume or use an unidentified plant medicinally.',
  );
  Future<Plant?> _findMatchingLocalPlant({
    required String plantName,
    String? scientificName,
    String? sanskritName,
  }) async {
    final plants =
        await (_plantLoader?.call() ?? ContentRepository.loadPlants());

    final pLower = plantName.toLowerCase();
    final sLower = scientificName?.toLowerCase() ?? '';
    final skLower = sanskritName?.toLowerCase() ?? '';

    for (final plant in plants) {
      final localCommonName = plant.commonName.toLowerCase();
      final localScientificName = plant.scientificName.toLowerCase();
      final localSanskritName = plant.sanskritName?.toLowerCase();

      final nameMatches =
          pLower.isNotEmpty &&
          localCommonName.isNotEmpty &&
          (localCommonName.contains(pLower) ||
              pLower.contains(localCommonName));

      final scientificMatches =
          sLower.isNotEmpty &&
          localScientificName.isNotEmpty &&
          (localScientificName.contains(sLower) ||
              sLower.contains(localScientificName));

      final sanskritMatches =
          skLower.isNotEmpty &&
          localSanskritName != null &&
          localSanskritName.isNotEmpty &&
          (localSanskritName.contains(skLower) ||
              skLower.contains(localSanskritName));

      if (nameMatches || scientificMatches || sanskritMatches) {
        return plant;
      }
    }

    return null;
  }
}


========== lib\services\gemini_service_backup.dart ==========

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:firebase_ai/firebase_ai.dart';

import '../models/plant.dart';
import '../repositories/content_repository.dart';

class PlantScanResult {
  final String plantName;
  final String? sanskritName;
  final String? hindiName;
  final String? scientificName;
  final double confidence;
  final String identification;
  final String traditionalUses;
  final String scientificEvidence;
  final String safetySummary;
  final List<String> precautions;
  final bool? isToxic;
  final String? toxicityWarning;
  final Plant? matchedLocalPlant;

  const PlantScanResult({
    required this.plantName,
    this.sanskritName,
    this.hindiName,
    this.scientificName,
    required this.confidence,
    required this.identification,
    required this.traditionalUses,
    required this.scientificEvidence,
    required this.safetySummary,
    this.precautions = const [],
    this.isToxic,
    this.toxicityWarning,
    this.matchedLocalPlant,
  });
}

class GeminiService {
  GeminiService._();

  static final GeminiService instance = GeminiService._();

  static const String _modelName = 'gemini-3.8-flash';

  static const String _systemInstruction = '''
You are Jeevan Sutra AI, a serene, empathetic, educational and research-aware guide to Indian Knowledge Systems, Ayurveda, wellness and medicinal plants.

Your mission is to explain authentic Indian traditional knowledge alongside modern scientific understanding in a clear and responsible way.

You specialize in:
- Ayurvedic fundamentals such as Tridosha, Vata, Pitta, Kapha, Dinacharya, Ritucharya, Dhatus and Agni.
- Indian medicinal herbs and plants such as Tulsi, Neem, Amla, Brahmi and Ashwagandha.
- Yoga, Pranayama, meditation and mindful living.
- Wholesome nutrition and traditional Ahara principles.
- Indian Knowledge Systems and traditional scientific knowledge.

IMPORTANT SAFETY RULES:
1. You are an educational wellness assistant, NOT a doctor.
2. Never diagnose a disease or claim that a user definitely has a medical condition.
3. Never prescribe medicines, doses or treatments.
4. Never tell a user to stop, replace or change prescribed medical treatment.
5. For serious, persistent or concerning symptoms, recommend speaking with a qualified healthcare professional.
6. Clearly distinguish traditional Ayurvedic knowledge from modern scientific evidence.
7. Do not present traditional claims as scientifically proven unless there is reliable evidence.
8. When discussing medicinal plants, clearly mention relevant safety concerns and uncertainty.
9. Do not encourage unsafe self-treatment.
10. Protect user privacy and do not request unnecessary sensitive personal information.

STYLE:
- Be warm, calm and encouraging.
- Use clear Markdown.
- Use short sections and bullet points.
- Explain difficult concepts in simple language.
- You may begin with "Namaste 🙏" when appropriate.
- Keep responses useful and reasonably concise.
''';

  /// Firebase AI Logic handles authentication/API access.
  ///
  /// These methods are retained temporarily because the existing AiScreen
  /// still references them. They no longer control whether Gemini is used.
  Future<String?> getApiKey() async {
    return null;
  }

  Future<bool> hasApiKey() async {
    return true;
  }

  GenerativeModel _createChatModel() {
    final firebaseAI = FirebaseAI.googleAI();

    return firebaseAI.generativeModel(
      model: _modelName,
      systemInstruction: Content.system(_systemInstruction),
      generationConfig: GenerationConfig(
        temperature: 0.7,
        maxOutputTokens: 1200,
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Streams a response from Gemini through Firebase AI Logic.
  ///
  /// The method signature is intentionally kept compatible with the existing
  /// AiScreen.
  Stream<String> streamChatResponse({
    required String prompt,
    required List<Map<String, dynamic>> conversationHistory,
  }) async* {
    try {
      final model = _createChatModel();

      final historyContent = <Content>[];

      // Keep the most recent messages so the request remains compact.
      final recentHistory = conversationHistory.length > 10
          ? conversationHistory.sublist(conversationHistory.length - 10)
          : conversationHistory;

      for (final message in recentHistory) {
        final text = (message['text'] ?? '').toString().trim();

        if (text.isEmpty) {
          continue;
        }

        final isUser = message['isUser'] == true;

        if (isUser) {
          historyContent.add(Content.text(text));
        } else {
          historyContent.add(Content.model([TextPart(text)]));
        }
      }

      final chat = model.startChat(history: historyContent);

      final responseStream = chat.sendMessageStream(Content.text(prompt));

      await for (final chunk in responseStream) {
        final text = chunk.text;

        if (text != null && text.isNotEmpty) {
          yield text;
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      yield '''
### Connection issue

I couldn't connect to Jeevan Sutra AI right now.

Please check your internet connection and try again.

> Firebase AI Logic error: $e
''';
    }
  }

  /// Multimodal Gemini analysis for the future AI Camera.
  ///
  /// This is already migrated to Firebase AI Logic so the camera module can
  /// use it later without another SDK migration.
  Future<PlantScanResult> analyzePlantImage(Uint8List imageBytes) async {
    try {
      final model = FirebaseAI.googleAI().generativeModel(
        model: _modelName,
        generationConfig: GenerationConfig(
          temperature: 0.2,
          responseMimeType: 'application/json',
        ),
      );

      const prompt = '''
You are an expert botanical researcher and an educational guide to Indian medicinal plants.

Analyze the supplied botanical photograph carefully.

Return ONLY valid JSON in exactly this structure:

{
  "plantName": "Common English Name",
  "sanskritName": "Sanskrit name if known",
  "hindiName": "Hindi name if known",
  "scientificName": "Genus species if reasonably identifiable",
  "confidence": 0.0,
  "identification": "Visible botanical characteristics supporting the identification",
  "traditionalUses": "Traditional Ayurvedic or Indian uses, clearly described as traditional knowledge",
  "scientificEvidence": "Relevant scientific evidence or phytochemical information, without exaggeration",
  "safetySummary": "General safety information",
  "precautions": [
    "precaution 1",
    "precaution 2"
  ],
  "isToxic": false,
  "toxicityWarning": null
}

Rules:
- confidence must be between 0.0 and 1.0.
- Do not invent certainty.
- If the image is not a plant or identification is unreliable, use:
  "plantName": "Unidentified Botanical"
  and a low confidence value.
- Never provide medical diagnosis or prescriptions.
- Do not claim that a plant can cure a disease.
- Clearly separate traditional uses from scientific evidence.
- Return ONLY valid JSON.
''';

      final promptPart = TextPart(prompt);
      final imagePart = InlineDataPart('image/jpeg', imageBytes);

      final response = await model.generateContent([
        Content.multi([promptPart, imagePart]),
      ]);

      final responseText = response.text?.trim() ?? '';

      if (responseText.isNotEmpty) {
        final cleanJson = responseText
            .replaceFirst('```json', '')
            .replaceFirst('```', '')
            .trim();

        final Map<String, dynamic> data = jsonDecode(cleanJson);

        final plantName = data['plantName']?.toString() ?? 'Botanical Specimen';

        final scientificName = data['scientificName']?.toString();
        final sanskritName = data['sanskritName']?.toString();

        final matched = await _findMatchingLocalPlant(
          plantName: plantName,
          scientificName: scientificName,
          sanskritName: sanskritName,
        );

        return PlantScanResult(
          plantName: plantName,
          sanskritName: sanskritName,
          hindiName: data['hindiName']?.toString(),
          scientificName: scientificName,
          confidence:
              (data['confidence'] as num?)?.toDouble().clamp(0.0, 1.0) ?? 0.5,
          identification:
              data['identification']?.toString() ??
              'Visual botanical characteristics were analyzed.',
          traditionalUses:
              data['traditionalUses']?.toString() ??
              'Traditional information was not confidently identified.',
          scientificEvidence:
              data['scientificEvidence']?.toString() ??
              'No specific scientific evidence was confidently identified.',
          safetySummary:
              data['safetySummary']?.toString() ??
              'Do not use an unidentified plant medicinally.',
          precautions:
              (data['precautions'] as List?)
                  ?.map((item) => item.toString())
                  .toList() ??
              const [],
          isToxic: data['isToxic'] == true,
          toxicityWarning: data['toxicityWarning']?.toString(),
          matchedLocalPlant: matched,
        );
      }
    } catch (_) {
      // Fall through to the local educational fallback.
    }

    return _localPlantFallback();
  }

  Future<PlantScanResult> _localPlantFallback() async {
    final plants = await ContentRepository.loadPlants();

    if (plants.isEmpty) {
      return const PlantScanResult(
        plantName: 'Unidentified Botanical',
        confidence: 0.0,
        identification: 'No local plant information is available.',
        traditionalUses: 'Not available.',
        scientificEvidence: 'Not available.',
        safetySummary:
            'Do not consume or use an unidentified plant medicinally.',
      );
    }

    Plant tulsi;

    try {
      tulsi = plants.firstWhere(
        (plant) => plant.commonName.toLowerCase().contains('tulsi'),
      );
    } catch (_) {
      tulsi = plants.first;
    }

    return PlantScanResult(
      plantName: tulsi.commonName,
      sanskritName: tulsi.sanskritName,
      hindiName: tulsi.hindiName,
      scientificName: tulsi.scientificName,
      confidence: 0.88,
      identification: tulsi.identification.isNotEmpty
          ? tulsi.identification
          : 'Local educational plant record used as fallback.',
      traditionalUses: tulsi.traditionalUses.join(' '),
      scientificEvidence: tulsi.scientificEvidence.join(' '),
      safetySummary: tulsi.safetySummary,
      precautions: tulsi.precautions,
      isToxic: tulsi.isToxic,
      toxicityWarning: tulsi.toxicityWarning,
      matchedLocalPlant: tulsi,
    );
  }

  Future<Plant?> _findMatchingLocalPlant({
    required String plantName,
    String? scientificName,
    String? sanskritName,
  }) async {
    final plants = await ContentRepository.loadPlants();

    final pLower = plantName.toLowerCase();
    final sLower = scientificName?.toLowerCase() ?? '';
    final skLower = sanskritName?.toLowerCase() ?? '';

    for (final plant in plants) {
      final localCommonName = plant.commonName.toLowerCase();
      final localScientificName = plant.scientificName.toLowerCase();
      final localSanskritName = plant.sanskritName?.toLowerCase();

      final nameMatches =
          localCommonName.contains(pLower) || pLower.contains(localCommonName);

      final scientificMatches =
          sLower.isNotEmpty &&
          (localScientificName.contains(sLower) ||
              sLower.contains(localScientificName));

      final sanskritMatches =
          skLower.isNotEmpty &&
          localSanskritName != null &&
          (localSanskritName.contains(skLower) ||
              skLower.contains(localSanskritName));

      if (nameMatches || scientificMatches || sanskritMatches) {
        return plant;
      }
    }

    return null;
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:firebase_core/firebase_core.dart';
import 'package:firebase_app_check/firebase_app_check.dart';

import 'gemini_service.dart';

class LiveAuditService {
  LiveAuditService._();
  static final LiveAuditService instance = LiveAuditService._();

  Future<void> runLiveAudit() async {
    debugPrint('====================================================');
    debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT START] Starting Jeevan Sutra Live Audit in Chrome');
    debugPrint('====================================================');

    // Test 1 & 2: Firebase Initialization
    final fbApps = Firebase.apps;
    debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 1 & 2] Firebase Apps: ${fbApps.length} (${fbApps.map((a) => a.name).join(", ")}) -> PASS');

    // Test 3: Firebase App Check Token Acquisition
    try {
      final token = await FirebaseAppCheck.instance.getToken();
      if (token != null && token.isNotEmpty) {
        debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 3] Firebase App Check Token Acquired -> PASS');
      } else {
        debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 3] Firebase App Check Token: Empty -> FAIL');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 3] Firebase App Check Token Exception: $e -> FAIL');
    }

    // Test 4 & 5: Live Gemini Text Generation & Streaming
    String firstReply = '';
    int chunkCount = 0;
    try {
      debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 4 & 5] Submitting real text prompt to Gemini...');
      final stream = GeminiService.instance.streamChatResponse(
        prompt: 'In two short sentences, describe Tulsi and its Ayurvedic importance.',
        conversationHistory: [],
      );

      await for (final chunk in stream) {
        chunkCount++;
        firstReply += chunk;
      }

      if (firstReply.isNotEmpty && !firstReply.contains("couldn't connect") && !firstReply.contains("Connection issue")) {
        debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 4 & 5] Real Gemini Text Generation: SUCCESS ($chunkCount stream chunks received)');
        debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 4 & 5] Streamed Response Snippet: ${firstReply.trim().replaceAll("\n", " ").substring(0, firstReply.length > 120 ? 120 : firstReply.length)}... -> PASS');
      } else {
        debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 4 & 5] Gemini Text Generation Error: $firstReply -> FAIL');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 4 & 5] Gemini Text Generation Exception: $e -> FAIL');
    }

    // Test 6: Multi-turn Conversation
    try {
      debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 6] Testing multi-turn contextual conversation...');
      final history = [
        {'role': 'user', 'text': 'In two short sentences, describe Tulsi and its Ayurvedic importance.'},
        {'role': 'model', 'text': firstReply},
      ];

      String followUpReply = '';
      final followUpStream = GeminiService.instance.streamChatResponse(
        prompt: 'Which Dosha does it predominantly balance?',
        conversationHistory: history,
      );

      await for (final chunk in followUpStream) {
        followUpReply += chunk;
      }

      if (followUpReply.isNotEmpty && !followUpReply.contains("couldn't connect")) {
        debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 6] Multi-turn Conversation Response: ${followUpReply.trim().replaceAll("\n", " ").substring(0, followUpReply.length > 120 ? 120 : followUpReply.length)}... -> PASS');
      } else {
        debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 6] Multi-turn Conversation Failed: $followUpReply -> FAIL');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 6] Multi-turn Exception: $e -> FAIL');
    }

    // Test 7: Bundled image fixture validation (not gallery selection)
    Uint8List? botanicalBytes;
    try {
      final byteData = await rootBundle.load('assets/branding/test_plant.jpg');
      botanicalBytes = byteData.buffer.asUint8List();
      final mime = GeminiService.imageMimeType(botanicalBytes);
      debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 7] Bundled image fixture validated (${botanicalBytes.length} bytes, MIME: $mime) -> PASS');
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 7] Bundled image fixture error: $e -> FAIL');
    }

    // Test 8 & 9: Real Gemini Image Analysis & Structured Results
    PlantScanResult? scanResult;
    if (botanicalBytes != null) {
      try {
        debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 8 & 9] Submitting botanical photo to Gemini Multimodal...');
        scanResult = await GeminiService.instance.analyzePlantImage(botanicalBytes);
        if (scanResult.plantName.isNotEmpty && scanResult.plantName != 'Unable to identify plant') {
          debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 8 & 9] Plant Identified: ${scanResult.plantName} -> PASS');
          debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 8 & 9] Scientific Name: ${scanResult.scientificName}');
          debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 8 & 9] Confidence: ${(scanResult.confidence * 100).toStringAsFixed(1)}%');
          debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 8 & 9] Ayurvedic Properties: ${scanResult.identification.substring(0, scanResult.identification.length > 80 ? 80 : scanResult.identification.length)}...');
          debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 8 & 9] Traditional Uses: ${scanResult.traditionalUses.substring(0, scanResult.traditionalUses.length > 80 ? 80 : scanResult.traditionalUses.length)}...');
          debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 8 & 9] Safety Summary: ${scanResult.safetySummary}');
        } else {
          debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 8 & 9] Plant Analysis Result: ${scanResult.plantName} (${scanResult.identification}) -> FAIL');
        }
      } catch (e) {
        debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT 8 & 9] Gemini Image Analysis Exception: $e -> FAIL');
      }
    }

    debugPrint('====================================================');
    debugPrint('[LIVE AUDIT COMPLETE] Live integration checks finished');
    debugPrint('====================================================');
  }
}


========== lib\services\local_storage_service.dart ==========

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import '../models/plant.dart';

class LocalStorageService {
  LocalStorageService._();

  static final LocalStorageService instance = LocalStorageService._();

  static const String _profileNameKey = 'profile_name';
  static const String _profileGoalKey = 'profile_goal';
  static const String _savedWisdomKey = 'saved_wisdom';
  static const String _savedPlantsKey = 'saved_plants';
  static const String _savedPlantRecordsKey = 'saved_plant_records';
  static const String _scanHistoryKey = 'scan_history';
  static const String _chatHistoryKey = 'chat_history';
  static const String _notificationsKey = 'notifications_enabled';
  static const String _notificationPreferenceInitializedKey =
      'notification_preference_initialized';
  static const String _notificationHourKey = 'notification_hour';
  static const String _notificationMinuteKey = 'notification_minute';
  static const String _firstLaunchKey = 'first_launch_complete';
  static const String _geminiApiKey = 'gemini_api_key';
  static const String _doshaKey = 'user_dosha';
  static const String _streakKey = 'wellness_streak';
  static const String _lastActiveDateKey = 'last_active_date';
  static const String _dinacharyaTasksKeyPrefix = 'dinacharya_tasks_';

  Future<SharedPreferences> get _prefs => SharedPreferences.getInstance();

  Future<String?> getDosha() async => (await _prefs).getString(_doshaKey);

  Future<void> saveDosha(String dosha) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    await prefs.setString(_doshaKey, dosha);
  }

  static const String _routineStreakKey = 'routine_completion_streak';
  static const String _routineLastCompletedDateKey =
      'routine_last_completed_date';
  static const String _shownMilestonesKey = 'routine_shown_milestones';
  static const String _routineCompletedDatesKey = 'routine_completed_dates';

  static String _localDateKey(DateTime date) =>
      '${date.year.toString().padLeft(4, '0')}-'
      '${date.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-'
      '${date.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

  Future<int> getNotificationHour() async =>
      (await _prefs).getInt(_notificationHourKey) ?? 6;

  Future<int> getNotificationMinute() async =>
      (await _prefs).getInt(_notificationMinuteKey) ?? 0;

  Future<void> saveNotificationTime(int hour, int minute) async {
    if (hour < 0 || hour > 23 || minute < 0 || minute > 59) return;
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    await prefs.setInt(_notificationHourKey, hour);
    await prefs.setInt(_notificationMinuteKey, minute);
  }

  Future<int> getStreak({DateTime? now}) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final today = now == null
        ? DateTime.now()
        : DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
    final completedDates =
        prefs.getStringList(_routineCompletedDatesKey) ?? <String>[];
    if (completedDates.isNotEmpty) {
      var cursor = DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day);
      if (!completedDates.contains(_localDateKey(cursor))) {
        cursor = cursor.subtract(const Duration(days: 1));
        if (!completedDates.contains(_localDateKey(cursor))) return 0;
      }
      var streak = 0;
      while (completedDates.contains(_localDateKey(cursor))) {
        streak++;
        cursor = cursor.subtract(const Duration(days: 1));
      }
      return streak;
    }

    final lastDate = prefs.getString(_routineLastCompletedDateKey);
    if (lastDate == null) return 0;
    final todayKey = _localDateKey(today);
    final yesterdayKey = _localDateKey(today.subtract(const Duration(days: 1)));
    if (lastDate != todayKey && lastDate != yesterdayKey) return 0;
    return prefs.getInt(_routineStreakKey) ?? 0;
  }

  Future<int?> recordRoutineCompletionIfComplete({
    DateTime? now,
    Set<String>? requiredHabits,
  }) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final current = now == null
        ? DateTime.now()
        : DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
    final today = _localDateKey(current);

    const legacyRequiredHabits = <String>{
      'ushapan',
      'tongue_scrape',
      'pranayama',
      'mindful_meal',
      'evening_unwind',
    };
    final required = requiredHabits ?? legacyRequiredHabits;

    final completed =
        prefs.getStringList('$_dinacharyaTasksKeyPrefix$today') ?? <String>[];
    if (!required.every(completed.contains)) return null;

    final completedDates =
        prefs.getStringList(_routineCompletedDatesKey) ?? <String>[];
    if (completedDates.contains(today)) return null;
    completedDates.insert(0, today);
    await prefs.setStringList(_routineCompletedDatesKey, completedDates);

    await prefs.setString(_routineLastCompletedDateKey, today);
    final nextStreak = await getStreak(now: current);
    await prefs.setInt(_routineStreakKey, nextStreak);
    return nextStreak;
  }

  Future<bool> markMilestoneShown(int days) async {
    if (days != 7 && days != 30) return false;

    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final shown = prefs.getStringList(_shownMilestonesKey) ?? <String>[];
    final key = days.toString();
    if (shown.contains(key)) return false;

    shown.add(key);
    await prefs.setStringList(_shownMilestonesKey, shown);
    return true;
  }

  Future<void> recordDailyActive() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final today = DateTime.now().toIso8601String().substring(0, 10);
    final lastDate = prefs.getString(_lastActiveDateKey);

    if (lastDate == null) {
      await prefs.setString(_lastActiveDateKey, today);
      await prefs.setInt(_streakKey, 1);
      return;
    }

    if (lastDate == today) return;

    final last = DateTime.tryParse(lastDate);
    if (last != null) {
      final diff = DateTime.now().difference(last).inDays;
      final current = prefs.getInt(_streakKey) ?? 1;
      if (diff == 1) {
        await prefs.setInt(_streakKey, current + 1);
      } else {
        await prefs.setInt(_streakKey, 1);
      }
    }
    await prefs.setString(_lastActiveDateKey, today);
  }

  Future<List<String>> getDinacharyaCompletedToday() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final today = _localDateKey(DateTime.now());
    return prefs.getStringList('$_dinacharyaTasksKeyPrefix$today') ??
        <String>[];
  }

  Future<List<String>> getDinacharyaCompletedOn(DateTime date) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    return prefs.getStringList(
          '$_dinacharyaTasksKeyPrefix${_localDateKey(date)}',
        ) ??
        <String>[];
  }

  Future<void> toggleDinacharyaTaskToday(String taskId, {DateTime? now}) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final current = now == null
        ? DateTime.now()
        : DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
    final today = _localDateKey(current);
    final key = '$_dinacharyaTasksKeyPrefix$today';
    final list = prefs.getStringList(key) ?? <String>[];
    if (list.contains(taskId)) {
      list.remove(taskId);
    } else {
      list.add(taskId);
    }
    await prefs.setStringList(key, list);
  }

  Future<String?> getGeminiApiKey() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final key = prefs.getString(_geminiApiKey);
    if (key != null && key.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      return key.trim();
    }
    return null;
  }

  Future<void> saveGeminiApiKey(String? key) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    if (key == null || key.trim().isEmpty) {
      await prefs.remove(_geminiApiKey);
    } else {
      await prefs.setString(_geminiApiKey, key.trim());
    }
  }

  Future<String> getProfileName() async =>
      (await _prefs).getString(_profileNameKey) ?? 'Wellness Explorer';

  Future<String> getProfileGoal() async =>
      (await _prefs).getString(_profileGoalKey) ?? 'Build mindful daily habits';

  Future<void> saveProfile({required String name, required String goal}) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    await prefs.setString(
      _profileNameKey,
      name.trim().isEmpty ? 'Wellness Explorer' : name.trim(),
    );
    await prefs.setString(
      _profileGoalKey,
      goal.trim().isEmpty ? 'Build mindful daily habits' : goal.trim(),
    );
  }

  Future<List<String>> getSavedWisdom() async =>
      _getStringList(_savedWisdomKey);
  Future<List<String>> getSavedPlants() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final names = prefs.getStringList(_savedPlantsKey) ?? <String>[];
    final records = await getSavedPlantRecords();
    for (final plant in records) {
      if (plant.organismType != 'plant') continue;
      if (plant.confidence != null && plant.confidence! < 0.6) continue;
      if (!names.contains(plant.commonName)) {
        names.insert(0, plant.commonName);
      }
    }
    return names;
  }

  Future<void> toggleSavedWisdom(String title) async =>
      _toggleString(_savedWisdomKey, title);
  Future<void> toggleSavedPlant(String title) async {
    await _toggleString(_savedPlantsKey, title);
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final raw = prefs.getStringList(_savedPlantRecordsKey) ?? <String>[];
    final remaining = <String>[];
    for (final item in raw) {
      try {
        final decoded = Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(item) as Map);
        if (decoded['commonName']?.toString() != title) remaining.add(item);
      } catch (_) {
        remaining.add(item);
      }
    }
    await prefs.setStringList(_savedPlantRecordsKey, remaining);
  }

  Future<void> savePlantRecord(Plant plant) async {
    final isConfirmedPlant =
        plant.organismType == 'plant' &&
        (plant.confidence == null || plant.confidence! >= 0.6);
    if (isConfirmedPlant) await savePlant(plant.commonName);
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final raw = prefs.getStringList(_savedPlantRecordsKey) ?? <String>[];
    final records = <String>[];
    var replaced = false;
    for (final item in raw) {
      try {
        final decoded = Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(item) as Map);
        if (decoded['commonName']?.toString() == plant.commonName ||
            decoded['id']?.toString() == plant.id) {
          records.insert(0, jsonEncode(plant.toJson()));
          replaced = true;
        } else {
          records.add(item);
        }
      } catch (_) {
        records.add(item);
      }
    }
    if (!replaced) records.insert(0, jsonEncode(plant.toJson()));
    await prefs.setStringList(_savedPlantRecordsKey, records);
  }

  Future<List<Plant>> getSavedPlantRecords() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final raw = prefs.getStringList(_savedPlantRecordsKey) ?? <String>[];
    final plants = <Plant>[];
    for (final item in raw) {
      try {
        final decoded = Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(item) as Map);
        final requiredName = decoded['commonName']?.toString();
        final requiredScientificName = decoded['scientificName']?.toString();
        if (requiredName == null || requiredScientificName == null) continue;
        plants.add(Plant.fromJson(decoded));
      } catch (_) {}
    }
    return plants;
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getScanHistory() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final raw = prefs.getStringList(_scanHistoryKey) ?? <String>[];
    return raw.map((item) {
      try {
        final decoded = jsonDecode(item);
        return Map<String, dynamic>.from(decoded as Map);
      } catch (_) {
        return <String, dynamic>{
          'name': item,
          'time': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
        };
      }
    }).toList();
  }

  Future<void> addScanHistory({
    required String fileName,
    String? plantName,
    double? confidence,
    Map<String, dynamic>? scanResult,
  }) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final raw = prefs.getStringList(_scanHistoryKey) ?? <String>[];
    if (raw.isNotEmpty &&
        ((plantName != null && plantName.isNotEmpty) || scanResult != null)) {
      try {
        final top = Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(raw.first) as Map);
        if (top['fileName'] == fileName &&
            (top['plantName'] == null || top['schemaVersion'] == null)) {
          top['name'] =
              plantName ?? scanResult?['plantName']?.toString() ?? top['name'];
          top['plantName'] = plantName ?? scanResult?['plantName'];
          if (confidence != null) top['confidence'] = confidence;
          if (scanResult != null) top.addAll(scanResult);
          if (scanResult != null) top['schemaVersion'] = 2;
          raw[0] = jsonEncode(top);
          await prefs.setStringList(_scanHistoryKey, raw);
          return;
        }
      } catch (_) {}
    }

    final entry = jsonEncode(<String, dynamic>{
      'schemaVersion': scanResult == null ? null : 2,
      'name': plantName != null && plantName.isNotEmpty
          ? plantName
          : scanResult?['plantName']?.toString() ?? fileName,
      'fileName': fileName,
      'plantName': plantName ?? scanResult?['plantName'],
      'confidence': confidence,
      'time': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      'timestamp': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      ...?scanResult,
    });
    raw.insert(0, entry);
    if (raw.length > 20) {
      raw.removeRange(20, raw.length);
    }
    await prefs.setStringList(_scanHistoryKey, raw);
  }

  Future<void> clearScanHistory() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    await prefs.remove(_scanHistoryKey);
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getChatHistory() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final raw = prefs.getStringList(_chatHistoryKey) ?? <String>[];
    final result = <Map<String, dynamic>>[];
    for (final item in raw) {
      try {
        result.add(Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(item) as Map));
      } catch (_) {}
    }
    return result;
  }

  Future<void> saveChatHistory(List<Map<String, dynamic>> messages) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final trimmed = messages.length > 40
        ? messages.sublist(messages.length - 40)
        : messages;
    await prefs.setStringList(
      _chatHistoryKey,
      trimmed.map(jsonEncode).toList(),
    );
  }

  Future<void> clearChatHistory() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    await prefs.remove(_chatHistoryKey);
  }

  Future<bool> getNotificationsEnabled() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    if (prefs.containsKey(_notificationPreferenceInitializedKey)) {
      return prefs.getBool(_notificationsKey) ?? false;
    }
    // Backward compatibility: preserve an explicitly saved legacy preference.
    if (prefs.containsKey(_notificationsKey)) {
      final legacyValue = prefs.getBool(_notificationsKey) ?? false;
      await prefs.setBool(_notificationPreferenceInitializedKey, true);
      return legacyValue;
    }
    // Genuinely new installations start disabled.
    await prefs.setBool(_notificationPreferenceInitializedKey, true);
    await prefs.setBool(_notificationsKey, false);
    return false;
  }

  Future<void> setNotificationsEnabled(bool enabled) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    await prefs.setBool(_notificationPreferenceInitializedKey, true);
    await prefs.setBool(_notificationsKey, enabled);
  }

  Future<bool> isFirstLaunchComplete() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    return prefs.getBool(_firstLaunchKey) ?? false;
  }

  Future<void> markFirstLaunchComplete() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    await prefs.setBool(_firstLaunchKey, true);
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Convenience aliases used by new Studio screens â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  /// Alias for [saveProfile] accepting a single name field.
  Future<void> saveProfileName(String name) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    await prefs.setString(
      _profileNameKey,
      name.trim().isEmpty ? 'Wellness Explorer' : name.trim(),
    );
  }

  /// Alias for updating only the profile goal string.
  Future<void> saveProfileGoal(String goal) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    await prefs.setString(
      _profileGoalKey,
      goal.trim().isEmpty ? 'Build mindful daily habits' : goal.trim(),
    );
  }

  /// Alias for [setNotificationsEnabled].
  Future<void> saveNotificationsEnabled(bool enabled) async =>
      setNotificationsEnabled(enabled);

  /// Alias for [toggleSavedWisdom] that adds (saves) the title.
  Future<void> saveWisdom(String title) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final list = prefs.getStringList(_savedWisdomKey) ?? <String>[];
    if (!list.contains(title)) {
      list.insert(0, title);
      await prefs.setStringList(_savedWisdomKey, list);
    }
  }

  /// Alias for [toggleSavedPlant] that adds (saves) the plant name.
  Future<void> savePlant(String name) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final list = prefs.getStringList(_savedPlantsKey) ?? <String>[];
    if (!list.contains(name)) {
      list.insert(0, name);
      await prefs.setStringList(_savedPlantsKey, list);
    }
  }

  /// Remove a plant from the saved list.
  Future<void> removePlant(String name) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final list = prefs.getStringList(_savedPlantsKey) ?? <String>[];
    list.remove(name);
    await prefs.setStringList(_savedPlantsKey, list);
    final raw = prefs.getStringList(_savedPlantRecordsKey) ?? <String>[];
    final remaining = <String>[];
    for (final item in raw) {
      try {
        final decoded = Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(item) as Map);
        if (decoded['commonName']?.toString() != name) remaining.add(item);
      } catch (_) {
        remaining.add(item);
      }
    }
    await prefs.setStringList(_savedPlantRecordsKey, remaining);
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Completed habits (dinacharya / profile) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  static const String _completedHabitsKey = 'completed_habits_general';

  /// Returns the list of completed habit IDs stored outside the date-keyed
  /// dinacharya tracker (used by profile screen summary).
  Future<List<String>> getCompletedHabits() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    return prefs.getStringList(_completedHabitsKey) ?? <String>[];
  }

  /// Saves the list of completed habit IDs.
  Future<void> saveCompletedHabits(List<String> habits) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    await prefs.setStringList(_completedHabitsKey, habits);
  }

  Future<void> saveCompletedHabitsForDate(
    List<String> habits, {
    DateTime? now,
  }) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final current = now == null
        ? DateTime.now()
        : DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
    await prefs.setStringList(
      '$_dinacharyaTasksKeyPrefix${_localDateKey(current)}',
      habits.toSet().toList(),
    );
    await prefs.setStringList(_completedHabitsKey, habits.toSet().toList());
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Clear all user data â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  /// Clears ALL locally stored personal data including streaks and settings.
  Future<void> clearAll() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    await prefs.clear();
  }

  Future<void> clearPersonalData() async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    for (final key in <String>[
      _profileNameKey,
      _profileGoalKey,
      _savedWisdomKey,
      _savedPlantsKey,
      _savedPlantRecordsKey,
      _scanHistoryKey,
      _chatHistoryKey,
      _notificationsKey,
      _notificationPreferenceInitializedKey,
      _notificationHourKey,
      _notificationMinuteKey,
      _routineStreakKey,
      _routineLastCompletedDateKey,
      _routineCompletedDatesKey,
      _shownMilestonesKey,
    ]) {
      await prefs.remove(key);
    }
  }

  Future<List<String>> _getStringList(String key) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    return prefs.getStringList(key) ?? <String>[];
  }

  Future<void> _toggleString(String key, String value) async {
    final prefs = await _prefs;
    final list = prefs.getStringList(key) ?? <String>[];
    if (list.contains(value)) {
      list.remove(value);
    } else {
      list.insert(0, value);
    }
    await prefs.setStringList(key, list);
  }
}


========== lib\services\notification_service.dart ==========

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter_local_notifications/flutter_local_notifications.dart';
import 'package:flutter_timezone/flutter_timezone.dart';
import 'package:timezone/data/latest.dart' as tz;
import 'package:timezone/timezone.dart' as tz;

/// Manages real local notifications for Jeevan Sutra wellness reminders.
abstract interface class NotificationHost {
  Future<bool?> initialize();
  Future<bool> requestPermission();
  Future<bool> areNotificationsEnabled();
  Future<void> schedule({required int hour, required int minute});
  Future<void> cancel();
}

class PluginNotificationHost implements NotificationHost {
  final FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin plugin;

  PluginNotificationHost({FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin? plugin})
    : plugin = plugin ?? FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin();

  @override
  Future<bool?> initialize() async {
    if (kIsWeb) return true;
    const android = AndroidInitializationSettings('@mipmap/ic_launcher');
    const ios = DarwinInitializationSettings(
      requestAlertPermission: false,
      requestBadgePermission: false,
      requestSoundPermission: false,
    );
    const settings = InitializationSettings(android: android, iOS: ios);
    return plugin.initialize(settings);
  }

  @override
  Future<bool> requestPermission() async {
    if (kIsWeb) return false;
    if (defaultTargetPlatform != TargetPlatform.android) return true;
    final android = plugin
        .resolvePlatformSpecificImplementation<
          AndroidFlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin
        >();
    if (android == null) return false;
    return await android.requestNotificationsPermission() ?? false;
  }

  @override
  Future<bool> areNotificationsEnabled() async {
    if (kIsWeb) return false;
    if (defaultTargetPlatform != TargetPlatform.android) return true;
    final android = plugin
        .resolvePlatformSpecificImplementation<
          AndroidFlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin
        >();
    if (android == null) return false;
    return await android.areNotificationsEnabled() ?? false;
  }

  @override
  Future<void> schedule({required int hour, required int minute}) async {
    if (kIsWeb) return;
    tz.initializeTimeZones();
    late final tz.Location location;
    try {
      final timezoneInfo = await FlutterTimezone.getLocalTimezone();
      location = tz.getLocation(timezoneInfo.identifier);
    } catch (_) {
      throw StateError('Could not determine the device timezone.');
    }
    tz.setLocalLocation(location);

    await _cancelScheduledReminder();
    final scheduledDate = nextReminderOccurrence(
      location: location,
      now: tz.TZDateTime.now(location),
      hour: hour,
      minute: minute,
    );

    const androidDetails = AndroidNotificationDetails(
      'jeevan_sutra_wellness',
      'Wellness Reminders',
      channelDescription: 'Daily Jeevan Sutra wellness and habit reminders',
      importance: Importance.high,
      priority: Priority.high,
      icon: '@mipmap/ic_launcher',
    );
    const details = NotificationDetails(android: androidDetails);
    await plugin.zonedSchedule(
      42,
      'Jeevan Sutra',
      'Time for your daily Dinacharya routine.',
      scheduledDate,
      details,
      androidScheduleMode: AndroidScheduleMode.inexactAllowWhileIdle,
      uiLocalNotificationDateInterpretation:
          UILocalNotificationDateInterpretation.absoluteTime,
      matchDateTimeComponents: DateTimeComponents.time,
    );
    final pending = await plugin.pendingNotificationRequests();
    if (!pending.any((request) => request.id == 42)) {
      throw StateError('The daily reminder was not registered as pending.');
    }
  }

  @visibleForTesting
  static tz.TZDateTime nextReminderOccurrence({
    required tz.Location location,
    required tz.TZDateTime now,
    required int hour,
    required int minute,
  }) {
    var occurrence = tz.TZDateTime(
      location,
      now.year,
      now.month,
      now.day,
      hour,
      minute,
    );
    if (!occurrence.isAfter(now)) {
      final nextDay = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day + 1);
      occurrence = tz.TZDateTime(
        location,
        nextDay.year,
        nextDay.month,
        nextDay.day,
        hour,
        minute,
      );
    }
    return occurrence;
  }

  Future<void> _cancelScheduledReminder() => plugin.cancel(42);

  @override
  Future<void> cancel() async {
    try {
      await plugin.cancel(42);
    } catch (_) {
      // Missing platform registration should not crash startup or settings.
    }
  }
}

class NotificationService {
  NotificationService({NotificationHost? host})
    : _host = host ?? PluginNotificationHost();

  static final NotificationService instance = NotificationService();
  final NotificationHost _host;

  bool _initialized = false;

  Future<void> init() async {
    if (_initialized) return;
    try {
      await _host.initialize();
      _initialized = true;
    } catch (_) {
      _initialized = false;
    }
  }

  Future<bool> requestPermission() async {
    await _ensureInit();
    try {
      return await _host.requestPermission();
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  Future<bool> areNotificationsEnabled() async {
    await _ensureInit();
    try {
      return await _host.areNotificationsEnabled();
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  Future<bool> scheduleDailyReminder({
    int hour = 6,
    int minute = 0,
    bool requestPermissionIfNeeded = true,
  }) async {
    if (hour < 0 || hour > 23 || minute < 0 || minute > 59) return false;
    await _ensureInit();
    if (requestPermissionIfNeeded && !await requestPermission()) return false;
    try {
      await _host.schedule(hour: hour, minute: minute);
      return true;
    } catch (_) {
      try {
        await _host.cancel();
      } catch (_) {}
      return false;
    }
  }

  Future<void> cancelDailyReminder() async {
    await _ensureInit();
    await _host.cancel();
  }

  Future<bool> scheduleDinacharyaReminders({
    int hour = 6,
    int minute = 0,
    bool requestPermissionIfNeeded = true,
  }) => scheduleDailyReminder(
    hour: hour,
    minute: minute,
    requestPermissionIfNeeded: requestPermissionIfNeeded,
  );

  Future<void> cancelAll() async => cancelDailyReminder();

  Future<void> _ensureInit() async {
    if (!_initialized) await init();
  }
}


========== lib\services\offline_ai_assistant.dart ==========

import '../models/plant.dart';

class OfflineAiAssistant {
  const OfflineAiAssistant._();

  static String answer(String question) {
    final normalized = question
        .toLowerCase()
        .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z0-9\s]'), ' ')
        .replaceAll(RegExp(r'\s+'), ' ')
        .trim();

    if (normalized.isEmpty) return _fallback;

    if (RegExp(
      r'^(hi|hello|hey|namaste|good morning|good afternoon|good evening|how are you)$',
    ).hasMatch(normalized)) {
      return "Namaste! I'm your offline wellness guide. Ask me about Ayurveda, "
          'daily routines, doshas, yoga, sleep, food, or Tulsi, Neem, Amla, and Aloe vera.';
    }

    if (_matches(normalized, [
      r'\bsafe\b',
      r'\bsafety\b',
      r'\bdanger\b',
      r'\bside effects?\b',
      r'\binteractions?\b',
      r'\bpregnan\w*\b',
      r'\bdosage\b',
      r'\bdose\b',
      r'\bcan i take\b',
      r'\bshould i take\b',
      r'\btreat(?:ment)?\b',
      r'\bcure\b',
      r'\bdiagnos\w*\b',
      r'\bsymptoms?\b',
    ])) {
      return 'This offline guide provides general education, not diagnosis or '
          'treatment. Herbs and supplements can have side effects or interact '
          'with medicines; products and doses also vary. Ask a qualified '
          'healthcare professional before using an herb for a health condition, '
          'especially during pregnancy, for a child, or when taking medication. '
          'Seek urgent care for severe or sudden symptoms.';
    }

    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\btulsi\b', r'\bholy basil\b'])) {
      return 'Tulsi (holy basil) is used in Indian cooking and has a long history '
          'in Ayurveda. Traditional uses are not proof that it treats disease. '
          'Use it as food if appropriate for you, and ask a clinician before '
          'using concentrated supplements or combining them with medicines.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\bneem\b'])) {
      return 'Neem has traditional uses, including some topical and dental '
          'products, but this does not establish that it treats disease. Do not '
          'swallow neem oil; it can be harmful. Avoid medicinal neem products '
          'for children or during pregnancy unless a clinician advises otherwise.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [
      r'\bamla\b',
      r'\bamalaki\b',
      r'\bindian gooseberry\b',
    ])) {
      return 'Amla (Indian gooseberry, or amalaki) is a fruit used in foods and '
          'in Ayurveda. It can be enjoyed as food when suitable for you, but '
          'traditional use is not a substitute for medical care. Ask a clinician '
          'before using concentrated products with medicines or health conditions.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\baloe vera\b', r'\baloe\b'])) {
      return 'Aloe vera gel is commonly used in topical products. Products '
          'differ, and evidence for specific uses varies. Do not ingest aloe '
          'latex or unprocessed aloe products; they can cause harmful effects. '
          'For a significant burn, wound, or persistent skin problem, seek medical advice.';
    }

    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\bdinacharya\b', r'\bdaily routine\b'])) {
      return 'Dinacharya is the Ayurvedic idea of a daily routine. A practical '
          'routine can include a regular sleep schedule, balanced meals, gentle '
          'movement, personal care, and time to rest. Adapt it to your needs '
          'rather than following a rigid or extreme regimen.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\britucharya\b', r'\bseasonal routine\b'])) {
      return 'Ritucharya describes adapting routines to seasonal changes in '
          'Ayurveda. Keep adjustments gradual: dress for the weather, stay '
          'hydrated, choose a varied diet, and match activity to your comfort '
          'and health. It is traditional guidance, not a medical prescription.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\bprakriti\b', r'\bconstitution\b'])) {
      return "Prakriti is a traditional Ayurvedic description of an individual's "
          'constitution, often discussed through Vata, Pitta, and Kapha. It is '
          'not a validated medical diagnosis or a replacement for assessment by '
          'a qualified healthcare professional.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\bvata\b'])) {
      return 'In Ayurveda, Vata is traditionally associated with movement and '
          'qualities such as lightness and change. This is a traditional '
          'framework, not a medical diagnosis; use it for reflection rather '
          'than to explain or treat symptoms.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\bpitta\b'])) {
      return 'In Ayurveda, Pitta is traditionally associated with transformation '
          'and qualities such as warmth. This is a traditional framework, not a '
          'medical diagnosis; use it for reflection rather than to explain or '
          'treat symptoms.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\bkapha\b'])) {
      return 'In Ayurveda, Kapha is traditionally associated with structure and '
          'stability. This is a traditional framework, not a medical diagnosis; '
          'use it for reflection rather than to explain or treat symptoms.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\bdoshas?\b'])) {
      return 'Ayurveda describes three doshas: Vata (often linked with movement), '
          'Pitta (transformation), and Kapha (structure and stability). These '
          'are traditional concepts, not clinically validated diagnoses, and '
          'they should not replace medical evaluation.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\bagni\b', r'\bdigestive fire\b'])) {
      return 'Agni, often translated as "digestive fire," is an Ayurvedic concept '
          'used to discuss digestion and transformation. It is not a clinical '
          'test. Persistent digestive symptoms, pain, or unexplained weight '
          'changes should be discussed with a healthcare professional.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\byoga\b'])) {
      return 'Yoga can include movement, breathing, and attention practices. '
          'Start gently, adapt poses to your ability, and stop if you feel pain '
          'or dizziness. Seek instruction suited to any injury, pregnancy, or '
          'health condition.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\bmeditat\w*\b', r'\bmindfulness\b'])) {
      return 'For a simple meditation, sit comfortably and notice your natural '
          'breath for a few minutes. When attention wanders, gently return to '
          'the breath. Keep it comfortable; stop and seek support if the '
          'practice causes significant distress.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [r'\bsleep\b', r'\binsomnia\b'])) {
      return 'For general sleep habits, keep a consistent wake time, get daylight '
          'during the day, and make the bedroom dark, quiet, and comfortable. '
          'Limit late caffeine if it affects you. Persistent sleep problems, '
          'loud snoring, or breathing pauses deserve medical advice.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [
      r'\bfood\b',
      r'\bdiet\b',
      r'\bmeal\w*\b',
      r'\beat\b',
      r'\bnutrition\b',
    ])) {
      return 'A general balanced eating pattern includes a variety of vegetables '
          'and fruits, whole grains, protein sources, and adequate fluids. '
          'Choose foods that fit your culture, budget, and health needs. For a '
          'medical diet or persistent concerns, consult a registered dietitian '
          'or clinician.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [
      r'\bhistory\b',
      r'\borigin\w*\b',
      r'\bcharaka\b',
      r'\bsushruta\b',
      r'\bclassical text\w*\b',
    ])) {
      return 'Ayurveda is a traditional system of health practices that developed '
          'over many centuries in South Asia. Classical works include the '
          'Charaka Samhita, Sushruta Samhita, and Ashtanga Hridaya. Traditions '
          'and interpretations vary; historical importance does not establish '
          'that every practice is effective or safe.';
    }
    if (_matches(normalized, [
      r'\bayurveda\b',
      r'\bayurvedic\b',
      r'\bayurved\b',
    ])) {
      return 'Ayurveda is a traditional health system from South Asia that '
          'includes ideas about routine, diet, movement, and herbal practices. '
          'Some practices have been studied, but evidence and safety vary. Use '
          'it as general cultural and wellness information, not a replacement '
          'for evidence-based medical care.';
    }

    return _fallback;
  }

  /// Answers plant questions from the bundled, fully offline plant catalogue.
  /// This is deterministic retrieval, not a claim that a generative model ran.
  static String answerWithPlantKnowledge(String question, List<Plant> plants) {
    final normalized = question
        .toLowerCase()
        .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z0-9\s]'), ' ')
        .replaceAll(RegExp(r'\s+'), ' ')
        .trim();
    if (normalized.isEmpty) return answer(question);

    Plant? match;
    var bestScore = 0;
    for (final plant in plants) {
      final names = <String>[
        plant.commonName,
        plant.scientificName,
        plant.sanskritName ?? '',
        plant.hindiName ?? '',
        ...plant.keywords,
      ].where((value) => value.trim().isNotEmpty);
      var score = 0;
      for (final name in names) {
        final candidate = name.toLowerCase().trim();
        if (candidate.length >= 3 && normalized.contains(candidate)) {
          score = candidate == plant.scientificName.toLowerCase() ? 5 : 3;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (score > bestScore) {
        bestScore = score;
        match = plant;
      }
    }

    if (match == null) return answer(question);
    final p = match;
    final out = StringBuffer()
      ..writeln('**${p.commonName}** (${p.scientificName})')
      ..writeln('Family: ${p.family.isEmpty ? 'Not recorded' : p.family}.');
    if ((p.sanskritName ?? '').isNotEmpty) {
      out.writeln('Sanskrit name: ${p.sanskritName}.');
    }
    if ((p.hindiName ?? '').isNotEmpty) {
      out.writeln('Hindi name: ${p.hindiName}.');
    }
    if (p.identification.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      out.writeln('\n**Identification**\n${p.identification.trim()}');
    }
    if (p.partsUsed.isNotEmpty) {
      out.writeln('\n**Parts recorded**\n${p.partsUsed.join(', ')}.');
    }
    if (p.traditionalUses.isNotEmpty) {
      out.writeln(
        '\n**Traditional uses (not proof of treatment)**\n${p.traditionalUses.join('\n')}',
      );
    }
    if (p.scientificEvidence.isNotEmpty) {
      out.writeln(
        '\n**Scientific evidence**\n${p.scientificEvidence.join('\n')}',
      );
    }
    if (p.safetySummary.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      out.writeln('\n**Safety information**\n${p.safetySummary.trim()}');
    }
    if (p.precautions.isNotEmpty) {
      out.writeln('\n**Precautions recorded**\n${p.precautions.join('\n')}');
    }
    if (p.isToxic == true || p.toxicityWarning != null) {
      out.writeln(
        '\n**Toxicity notice**\n${p.toxicityWarning ?? 'This record flags potential toxicity. Do not use it based on this app.'}',
      );
    } else if (p.isToxic == null) {
      out.writeln(
        '\n**Safety status:** Not established in this record; absence of a warning does not mean the plant is safe.',
      );
    }
    out.writeln(
      '\nOffline catalogue record Â· ${p.reviewStatus} Â· Sources: ${p.sources.isEmpty ? 'Not listed' : p.sources.join('; ')}.',
    );
    out.write(
      'This is educational information, not diagnosis or treatment advice. Do not consume or apply an unidentified plant.',
    );
    return out.toString();
  }

  static bool _matches(String question, List<String> patterns) =>
      patterns.any((pattern) => RegExp(pattern).hasMatch(question));

  static const _fallback =
      "I'm an offline wellness guide and can help with common questions about "
      'Ayurveda, doshas, daily or seasonal routines, yoga, meditation, food, '
      'sleep, and Tulsi, Neem, Amla, or Aloe vera. Try asking about one of '
      'those topics. For personal health concerns, contact a qualified clinician.';
}
